### [74] [Open versioned aggregates of the FAA National Wildlife Strike Database (1990-2026): conditional damage rates by animal family, airframe, and airport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12390)
*Denys Kolomiiets*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 本研究基于美国联邦航空管理局（FAA）的野生动物撞击数据库，构建了可公开访问的SQLite版本，并对1990至2026年的飞机与野生动物碰撞事件进行了系统分析，重点评估不同物种、机场和航空器类型的损害率。研究采用分层聚合方法，按年份、机场、物种等维度整合数据，发现鸣禽类虽撞击频次高但损害率低，而鹿类撞击虽少但损害率极高。大型机场报告数量多但损害率相对较低，可能反映其报告更全面。结果显示，标记为“造成损害”的事件比例呈下降趋势，表明轻微事件报告增加，而实际造成损害的事件在2025年达峰值。研究成果以CC BY 4.0协议开放共享，支持后续独立研究复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 野生动物与飞机的碰撞是航空安全的重要威胁，但FAA数据库缺乏标准化、版本化的公开聚合数据，限制了独立研究与趋势分析。因此，有必要构建结构化、可复用的数据集，并系统评估不同因素下的损害率，以支持更精准的风险评估与政策制定。

Method: 将FAA公开的STRIKE_REPORTS数据转换为SQLite格式，定义DAMAGE_LEVEL为M、M?、S、D的报告为造成损害事件，利用SPECIES_ID的层级前缀对数据按年份、机场、物种、航空器类型和动物科进行聚合。分析涵盖所有民用航班及商业航班子集（AC_MASS 3-5），统计以每起报告为基础的条件损害率，趋势分析使用1990–2025年完整数据，横断面分析覆盖1990–2026年。

Result: 截至2026年7月4日数据冻结，2025年报告撞击事件达历史最高的24,458起（其中901起造成损害）。鸣禽类占已识别撞击事件的41.1%，但损害率仅为1.5%（95% CI 1.4–1.6），而鹿类仅占0.7%，损害率高达81.8%（95% CI 79.8–83.7）。按总撞击数排名靠前的机场（如丹佛国际机场）与按损害事件排名者（如萨克拉门托国际机场）差异显著；在至少有50份报告的452个机场中，报告量与条件损害率呈负相关（Spearman rho = -0.44，p < 0.001）。标记为损害的报告比例长期下降，但绝对损害数量在2025年达到峰值。

Conclusion: 通过构建版本化、开放获取的数据聚合集和透明的损害率分析框架，本研究支持对航空野生动物撞击事件的独立复现与深入研究。结果揭示了高频率与高风险物种之间的不一致性，提示不应仅以总撞击数评估风险。数据与方法以CC BY 4.0许可发布，促进科学共享与持续监测。

Abstract: Background. Aircraft-wildlife collisions are a documented aviation hazard. The FAA National Wildlife Strike Database is the main public source of U.S. reports, but raw extracts are large and rarely released as versioned aggregates.
  Methods. We convert the public FAA Access export (STRIKE_REPORTS) to SQLite, define a report as damaging when DAMAGE_LEVEL is one of M, M?, S, D, and fold rows into open aggregates (year, airport, species, aircraft, animal family) via hierarchical SPECIES_ID prefixes. We analyze all civil reports and a commercial subset (AC_MASS 3-5). Rates are per reported strike, not per flight hour, so every rate is conditional on a report having been filed. Trend statistics use complete incident years 1990-2025; cross-sectional facets cover the full seed, incident years 1990-2026.
  Results. Numbers are locked to the 2026-07-04 freeze. Reported strikes in 2025 reach a series high (24,458 total; 901 damaging). Species that dominate strike counts are not those with the highest damage rates: perching songbirds account for 41.1% of identified strikes but damage aircraft in 1.5% of reports (95% CI 1.4-1.6), whereas deer account for 0.7% but damage aircraft in 81.8% of reports (95% CI 79.8-83.7). Airports ranked by total strikes (led by Denver Intl) diverge from rankings by damaging strikes (led by Sacramento Intl); across the 452 airports with at least 50 reports, volume and conditional damage rate are negatively rank-correlated (Spearman rho = -0.44, p < 0.001); large stations plausibly capture minor events more completely, so this is not a hazard ranking. The share of reports labeled damaging declines over decades, consistent with improved reporting of minor events, while absolute damaging counts peak in 2025.
  Conclusions. Versioned open aggregates and a documented damage-rate analysis enable independent reuse; released under CC BY 4.0.

</details>


### [75] [Beyond persuasion: Mobile electoral interfaces in polarized societies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13184)
*Nuno Crokidakis,Celia Anteneodo*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种最小动力学模型，强调在极化选举中，选民流动性而非直接说服是决定选举结果的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释当代极化社会中稳定党派集团与流动选民并存的现象，揭示选举动态的新机制。

Method: 将 electorate 分为两个稳定党派集团和一个流动群体，通过费米型概率规则分配流动选民，抑制两极间直接转换，并进行解析计算。

Result: 发现选举对外界冲击的敏感性正比于流动选民界面的稳态规模，识别出流动性是控制宏观响应的关键量。

Conclusion: 在高度极化的选举中，总体变化主要由流动选民界面的波动驱动，而非大规模意识形态转变。

Abstract: Many traditional models of electoral dynamics emphasize opinion change, social influence and persuasion mechanisms. In contrast, contemporary polarized societies often exhibit relatively stable partisan blocs coexisting with an electorally relevant mobile population. We propose a minimal dynamical model in which electoral competition is governed not by direct persuasion between opposing blocs, but by the dynamics and allocation of a mobile electoral interface. The electorate is partitioned into two stable partisan blocs and a mobile fraction, while direct transitions between the polarized blocs are strongly suppressed. Mobile voters are allocated between the competing blocs through a Fermi-like probabilistic rule governed by rejection asymmetries. Analytical calculations show that the electoral susceptibility to political shocks is proportional to the stationary size of the mobile electoral interface, identifying electoral mobility as the key quantity controlling the macroscopic response of polarized systems to external perturbations. The model naturally predicts a continuum of mobility regimes, ranging from frozen polarization to highly responsive electoral states characterized by a broad mobile interface. These results suggest that, in strongly polarized elections, aggregate electoral changes may be governed primarily by fluctuations at the mobile electoral interface rather than by large-scale ideological conversion.

</details>

### [76] [Exponential multi-graylevel computational-weighted dithering for high-quality binarized Fourier single-pixel imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12958)
*Qigao Zhu,Haojia Jiang,Guan Wang,Lianhao Zhang,Hanlei Gong,Huaxia Deng,Xinglong Gong*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于指数多灰度级计算加权抖动的傅里叶单像素成像二值化方法，有效降低了量化误差，显著提升了成像质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的Floyd-Steinberg空间抖动在二值化傅里叶图案时引入较大量化误差，导致成像质量下降，本文旨在通过新抖动方法减少误差。

Method: 采用指数多灰度级计算加权抖动方法，将傅里叶图案量化为$2^R$个灰度级后分解为二值图案，优化二值化过程。

Result: 傅里叶系数的平均绝对百分比误差从194%降至28%，重建图像的结构相似性从0.430提升至0.971，分辨率接近瑞利判据理论极限。

Conclusion: 所提方法显著优于传统方法，有效提升二值化傅里叶单像素成像的质量和精度。

Abstract: Binarized Fourier single-pixel imaging (FSI) takes full advantage of the high modulation speed of digital micromirror devices by applying Floyd-Steinberg spatial dithering to binarize grayscale Fourier patterns. However, the use of the spatial dithering introduces substantial quantization errors, leading to decreasing imaging quality. Here, we propose a binarization method for grayscale Fourier patterns based on exponential multi-graylevel computational-weighted dithering, aimed at reducing quantization errors and then enhancing the imaging quality of binarized FSI. The proposed method quantizes Fourier patterns into $2^{R}$ values $\{0, 1/(R-1), 2/(R-1)... 1\}$ and then decomposes them into binarized patterns. Both simulation and experimental results demonstrate that the method significantly reduces quantization errors in Fourier coefficients acquisition and improves imaging quality. The mean absolute percentage error of Fourier coefficients decreases from $194\%$ to $28\%$ and the structural similarity of reconstructed images ($256\times256$ pixels) improves from 0.430 to 0.971, a $126\%$ enhancement compared to the conventional method. Lateral resolution of this proposed method almost approaches the theoretical lateral resolution limit calculated by Rayleigh Criterion.

</details>


### [77] [ProbSplat: Efficient Probabilistic Hardware for Gaussian Splatting in 3D Scene Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13143)
*Siddarth Gottumukkula,M P Samartha,Vedant Pahariya,Priyanshi Jain,Amit Ranjan Trivedi,Priyesh Shukla*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: ProbSplat是一种基于计算内存（CIM）的架构，利用浮栅反相器列实现高效的概率计算，适用于边缘端的机器人和AR/VR场景重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为提升边缘设备上高斯混合模型计算的能效与精度，解决传统数字实现中功耗高、计算复杂度大等问题。

Method: 采用可编程浮栅MOSFET结构，独立调控高斯分布的均值与方差，并在180nm CMOS工艺下实现硬件仿真，完成对数似然推理。

Result: 系统在3-D高斯混合建模中均值-方差偏差小于2.4%，每次推理仅消耗18pJ能量，支持500个混合函数；场景重建达到21.99 dB PSNR。

Conclusion: ProbSplat显著降低了计算复杂度、内存占用和功耗，为边缘智能应用提供了高效、可扩展的概率计算解决方案。

Abstract: This paper presents ProbSplat, a Compute-in-Memory (CIM)-inspired architecture based on programmable and energy efficient floating-gate inverter columns for probabilistic computing. Improving upon our prior work, ProbSplat programs and stores both means and variances of Gaussian mixture components, and evaluates log-likelihood for gaussian splatting during scene reconstruction with high energy efficiency, suitable for robotics and augmented/virtual reality (AR/VR) at the edge. Our proposed scheme enables independent control of both mean and variance via deterministic adjustment of floating-gate MOSFET threshold voltages, increasing the fidelity of hardware to program probability distributions. The design is simulated in 180nm CMOS on 1.8 V at 50 MHz and achieves mean-variance independence with <2.4% deviation during 3-D Gaussian mixture modeling. Compared to conventional digital implementations, ProbSplat significantly reduces compute complexity, memory footprint, and power consumption. The scalable framework consumes 18pJ energy per log-likelihood inference with 4-bit precision while operating for 500 mixture functions in a 3-D GMM. Scene reconstruction with ProbSplat's characteristics gave satisfactory fidelity of 21.99 PSNR (dB) at 8-bit precision.

</details>


### [78] [Enabling Ultra-Low-Power Always-On Feedforward Leakage Suppression Logic Circuits with FDSOI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13189)
*Clément Choné,Leslie Xu,Filippo Quadri,Pasquale Davide Schiavone,Alexandre Levisse,Jean-Luc Naguel,David Atienza,Andreas Burg*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 本文研究了在22纳米FDSOI技术下，采用馈电漏电抑制逻辑（FLSL）以降低始终开启电路的漏电功耗，显著提升了低占空比实时应用中的能效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对物联网和可穿戴设备中实时应用对能耗和时序的严格要求，传统低阈值晶体管在先进工艺节点下面临漏电增加的问题，亟需更高效的始终开启电路设计。

Method: 利用FDSOI技术抑制结漏电的优势，实现FLSL架构，并在22纳米FDSOI工艺上对FIR滤波器和AES加密核心进行硅片测量验证。

Result: 相比HVT和UHVT CMOS设计，FLSL在22纳米FDSOI下实现了工作电压降低，并将漏电功耗最多分别降低了9.8倍和1.83倍。

Conclusion: FLSL结合FDSOI技术可有效恢复先进工艺节点下的漏电抑制能力，为低频输入的小型始终开启电路提供了高能效解决方案。

Abstract: The growing deployment of real-time applications on wearable and Internet of Things (IoT) edge devices has intensified the need for energy-efficient, high-performance systems that meet stringent timing and energy constraints. Events-driven architectures leverage the sparsity of real-time to further improve system energy efficiency by employing an always-on (AO) domain to monitor inputs and activate a high-performance (HP) domain only when relevant events occur. However, for low-duty-cycle applications, the energy bottleneck shifts toward the AO domain, where leakage power dominates overall consumption. To mitigate this issue, AO circuits are typically implemented using high voltage threshold (HVT) or ultra-high voltage threshold (UHVT) transistors, thereby avoiding sub- and near-threshold operation, which is highly sensitive to process, voltage, and temperature (PVT) variations. In this context, feedforward leakage suppression logic (FLSL) has recently emerged as a promising candidate, offering reduced leakage compared to conventional. However, previous studies report a significant degradation in FLSL leakage performance in technology nodes below 90 nm, primarily due to increased gate and junction leakage currents. FDSOI technology, with its ability to effectively suppress junction leakage, provides an opportunity to overcome this limitation and restore FLSL efficiency in advanced nodes. Therefore, we demonstrate in this work that FLSL implemented in a 22 nm FDSOI technology can significantly reduce the energy consumption of small AO circuits with low-frequency inputs compared to state-of-the-art ultra-low-power CMOS designs. Silicon measurements on an FIR filter and an AES cryptographic core show reduced operating voltage and up to 9.8 x and 1.83 x reductions in leakage power compared to equivalent HVT and UHVT CMOS implementations, respectively.

</details>

### [79] [ARIES-Mission2: A Zero-Shot Vision-Language-Action Framework for Fast Large-Scale Aerial Mission Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12763)
*Junhao Wei,Yanxiao Li,Haochen Li,Yifu Zhao,Dexing Yao,Baili Lu,Zikun Li,Yapeng Wang,Sio-Kei Im,Dingcheng Yang,Xu Yang*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: ARIES-Mission2是一个零样本视觉-语言-动作框架，用于低空无人机任务生成，通过解耦视觉语义感知与物理路径优化，显著减少飞行距离并提升规划效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大模型在低空无人机任务生成中存在空间优化能力弱和路径规划效率低的问题，难以满足实际应用需求。

Method: 该框架首先利用DeepSeek-V3进行任务解析，使用Molmo-7B实现零样本目标定位，并通过地理空间插值将像素位置转换为GPS航点；后端将多目标巡航建模为旅行商问题（TSP），比较VLM原始顺序及PSO、GPSO、IPSO优化的路线，选择成本最低的闭环路径。

Result: 在UAV-VLPA-nano-30基准上，ARIES-Mission2总飞行距离为62.43公里，相比未优化基线减少21.6%，相比人工规划减少9.5%；全流程耗时575.40秒，单任务平均19.18秒，约为人工速度的3.6倍；TSP求解器每任务仅需0.16秒，且计算时间随目标数增长更平稳。

Conclusion: ARIES-Mission2有效结合了大模型的语义理解能力和传统优化算法的空间规划优势，在保证语义准确性的同时大幅提升路径效率和可扩展性，适用于复杂自然语言指令驱动的无人机自主任务生成。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have shown strong semantic understanding capabilities, but their direct use in low-altitude Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) mission generation remains limited by weak spatial optimization and inefficient route planning. To address this issue, we propose ARIES-Mission2, a zero-shot Vision-Language-Action (VLA) framework that decouples visual-semantic perception from physical route optimization. Given natural-language instructions and satellite imagery, ARIES-Mission2 first uses DeepSeek-V3 for task parsing and Molmo-7B for zero-shot target grounding, and then converts detected pixel locations into GPS waypoints through geospatial interpolation. To reduce the redundant backtracking caused by raw VLM-generated visiting orders, the back end formulates multi-target UAV traversal as a Traveling Salesperson Problem (TSP) and compares four candidate routes, including the raw VLM order and the routes optimized by PSO, GPSO, and IPSO. The minimum-cost closed-loop route is then selected for mission generation. Experiments on the UAV-VLPA-nano-30 benchmark show that ARIES-Mission2 achieves a total flight distance of 62.43 km, reducing the route length by 21.6% compared with the unoptimized VLA baseline (79.66 km) and by 9.5% compared with manual human planning (69.00 km). The complete 30-task workflow takes 575.40 s, averaging 19.18 s per task, which is approximately 3.6 times faster than human expert planning. Component-level timing shows that VLM inference dominates the runtime with 19.02 s per task, while the TSP solver requires only 0.16 s per task. Scalability analysis further indicates that the TSP module maintains lower growth in computation time as the number of targets increases.

</details>


### [80] [Julia for CFD: A Critical Survey of Ecosystem, Performance, and Composability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12801)
*Tianbai Xiao*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文综述了Julia语言在计算流体力学（CFD）中的应用，探讨其在性能、可扩展性、加速器移植性、自动微分和软件集成方面的优势与局限。Julia通过共享类型、分派和特化机制实现了多种功能的统一集成，已在大型分布式CPU和多GPU平台上实现可信的原生CFD模拟，并支持可微分工作流。然而，相较于成熟的C/C++/Fortran生态，Julia在工具链广度、工业级工具和部署经验方面仍有不足，目前最适合作为连接仿真与后续分析的新架构开发与测试平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着CFD越来越多地融入设计、推断、优化和数据驱动建模等工作流，对连接物理模型、数值核、异构硬件、微分和学习的需求日益增长。Julia提供了一种独特的高层科学抽象与高性能专用化相结合的方式，本文旨在评估该模式在CFD软件中的适用性与局限。

Method: 本文采用关键性综述方法，分析代表性开源项目，并综合应用层面的证据，评估Julia在性能、可扩展性、加速器移植性、自动微分和软件组合方面的能力，同时与C++框架、有限元领域特定语言及JAX-based CFD进行比较。

Result: 结果显示Julia已能在大型分布式CPU和多GPU平台实现原生CFD模拟，并支持新兴的可微分工作流；其核心优势在于通过共享类型、动态派发和编译期特化实现多种技术的无缝集成；但在生态系统广度、工业级工具和实际部署经验上仍落后于传统语言环境。

Conclusion: Julia在CFD中已超越概念验证阶段，在多个领域展现出可行性，尤其适合作为集成仿真与下游分析的新型架构研发平台，但尚未能全面替代C/C++/Fortran在工业界的主导地位。

Abstract: Modern CFD increasingly places simulation inside workflows for design, inference, optimization, and data-driven modeling, creating pressure to connect physical models, numerical kernels, heterogeneous hardware, differentiation, and learning. Julia offers a distinctive approach: high-level scientific abstractions can be specialized for performance and composed within a common language and compiler ecosystem. This critical survey examines where that model benefits CFD software and where its limits remain. We review representative open-source projects and synthesize application-level evidence on performance, scalability, accelerator portability, automatic differentiation, and software composition. Published results demonstrate credible Julia-native CFD on large distributed CPU systems and multi-GPU platforms, as well as emerging differentiable workflows. Comparisons with C++ performance-portability frameworks, finite-element domain-specific languages, and JAX-based differentiable CFD show that these capabilities are not unique to Julia. Julia's distinction is their integration through shared types, dispatch, and specialization. The evidence is mixed: Julia has progressed beyond proof of concept in several CFD regimes, but still lacks the ecosystem breadth, industrial tooling, and deployment experience of established C/C++/Fortran environments. Its strongest current role is as a platform for developing and testing CFD architectures that connect simulation with downstream analysis.

</details>


### [81] [Spinal Coupling in Frontal and Transversal Plane During Gait - A Segmental and Time-Dependent Analysis of the Thoracic and Lumbar Spine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12945)
*Jonas Dully,Carlo Dindorf,Henriette Rönsch,Dennis Perchthaler,Jürgen Konradi,Ulrich Betz,Michael Fröhlich*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本研究首次在行走过程中对T3至L4椎体水平的脊柱旋转与侧屈耦合行为进行了动态、逐椎体的量化分析，发现其具有头尾方向的变化模式，并受矢状面姿势显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脊柱侧屈与轴向旋转之间的耦合是脊柱力学的已知特征，但在步行过程中各椎体水平上的具体表现及其与矢状面姿势的关系尚不清楚。因此，本研究旨在明确个体椎体水平上的耦合行为及其受静态矢状面姿势的影响。

Method: 采用非侵入性光栅立体成像系统结合仪器化跑步机，对642名受试者的脊柱运动学进行分析，量化行走中T3至L4每个椎体的旋转与侧屈的时间依赖性耦合；通过傅里叶级数提取绝对相位滞后、归一化有符号面积和倾斜角来评估耦合行为。

Result: 三种指标均显示出明显的头尾向变化模式，相邻椎体间大多存在差异，并具有不同的转折点；总体及汇总的静态矢状面姿势显著调节这些模式，个体在静态姿势上的偏差也影响耦合指标。

Conclusion: 研究提供了步态中脊柱耦合在椎体水平上的首个动态特征描述，揭示了静态矢状面姿势对该耦合的重要影响，为理解脊柱生物力学机制及其在脊柱疾病诊断与治疗中的应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: Introduction: Coupling between lateral deviation and axial rotation is a known feature of spinal mechanics, yet its behavior at the individual vertebral level during gait, as well as the association with sagittal posture remains poorly understood. Methods: This study analyzed spinal kinematics in a diverse cohort (n=642) using a non-invasive rasterstereography system with an instrumented treadmill, quantifying time-dependent coupling of rotation and lateral deviation for each vertebra from T3 to L4 during walking as well as the influence of sagittal posture on this coupling. Coupling behavior was analyzed with absolute phase lag, normalized signed area and tilt angle derived from the Fourier series. Results: Results revealed cranio-caudal patterns for all three metrics with differences between almost all adjacent vertebrae and different turning points, i.e., where the coupling behavior changed (from increase to decrease and vice versa). Generalized, as well as pooled static sagittal posture significantly modulated these patterns, while individual deviations from static sagittal posture influenced the metrics. Conclusion: These findings provide the first dynamic, vertebra-level characterization of spinal coupling during gait. Furthermore, they offer a foundation for an understanding of spinal biomechanics and the influence of static sagittal posture, potentially relevant to the diagnosis and treatment of spinal disorders.

</details>


### [82] [TIEM: Temporal Integration of Hypergraph Evidence and Skill Memory for Event-Driven Financial Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13024)
*Wenjin Liu,Shen Pang,Tiesunlong Shen,Zhe Cui,Xiaobao Wu,Anh Tuan Luu,Haoran Luo*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于时间戳门控的框架TIEM，用于事件驱动的催化剂-结果预测，通过构建事件-证据超图、基于案例的技能记忆和异构证据-经验融合推理来解决训练数据污染和时间泄漏问题，并在多个金融预测基准上表现出优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于大语言模型的预测方法存在训练数据污染和时间泄漏问题，导致报告准确率与真实预测能力之间存在差距。为了解决这一问题，本文提出了TIEM框架。

Method: TIEM框架包含三个部分：1）事件-证据超图（EEH），实现基于时间戳过滤的多层级检索；2）案例式技能记忆（CSM），用于存储带源标签的时间技能；3）异构证据-经验融合推理（HEFR），实现证据与经验的融合并进行预测。同时引入FinPURE作为近期A股保留基准，并使用名称-日期探针评估模型对名称和日期敏感性的实际表现。

Result: 在五个金融预测基准上的实验结果表明，TIEM优于当前的基线方法。此外，通过FinPURE和名称-日期探针验证了模型的有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: TIEM能够有效缓解证据鸿沟问题，在事件驱动的金融预测任务中展现出更强的真实预测能力，具有良好的应用前景。

Abstract: Event-driven catalyst-outcome forecasting increasingly uses retrieval- and memory-augmented large language model agents for prediction. However, training-data contamination and temporal leakage can create an Evidence Chasm between reported accuracy and true predictive ability. We propose TIEM, a timestamp-gated framework with three coordinated components: an Event-Evidence Hypergraph (EEH) for timestamp-filtered multi-tier retrieval; a Case-based Skill Memory (CSM) for source-tagged temporal skills; and Heterogeneous Evidence-Experience Fusion Reasoning (HEFR) for evidence-experience fusion and prediction. We also introduce FinPURE, a recent-period A-share holdout benchmark, and use a Name-Date Probe to assess per-model name-date sensitivity rather than assuming training cutoffs. Results on five financial forecasting benchmarks show TIEM outperforms current baselines. Our project is available at https://github.com/QwenQKing/Fin_TIEM.

</details>

### [83] [Graph-theoretic design of lasing networks for physical vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13097)
*Paul Obernolte,Jakub Dranczewski,Yixiu Yin,Tobias Farchy,Wai Kit Ng,Tobias Simonsen,Elias Großhauser,Alexis Arnaudon,Robert L. Peach,Mauricio Barahona,Riccardo Sapienza,Jack C. Gartside,T. V. Raziman*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于图论度量的高效优化方法，用于设计具有复杂非线性动力学的物理神经网络，通过抽象图空间搜索实现比直接物理模拟快3000倍的优化速度，并显著提升图像分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于物理神经网络涉及复杂的多体物理和高昂的实验成本，直接在高维拓扑空间中进行优化不可行，因此需要一种更高效的优化框架。

Method: 建立了一个三层关联：图论度量预测非线性激光物理，进而预测视觉性能；利用该关系指导基于图度量的进化算法，在抽象图空间中高效搜索最优网络拓扑。

Result: 在模拟图像分类任务中，经过图优化的网络显著优于随机设计，且相比全物理模拟优化实现了约3000倍的速度提升。

Conclusion: 该框架不依赖特定物理实现，可推广至其他基于网络的物理学习系统，为强相互作用物理神经网络的设计提供了高效路径。

Abstract: Physical neural networks perform learning through the intrinsic nonlinear dynamics of matter. Optimising their design presents a considerable challenge: complex many-body physics can provide powerful computation, but are expensive to simulate and large experimental optimisation runs are impractical to fabricate. Hence, the high-dimensional space of possible network topologies cannot be effectively directly searched. Here, we show that this search can be efficiently performed in an abstract graph space that is vastly cheaper to explore. Using random lasing networks -- composed of interconnected nanoscale waveguides and hosting strongly coupled lasing modes -- as an exemplar physical vision system, we establish a quantitative three-layer link: simple graph-theoretic metrics predict the nonlinear lasing physics, which in turn predicts vision performance. After validating this relationship using physical simulations, we exploit it to drive an evolutionary algorithm using graph metrics, producing network topologies that outperform random designs at a fraction of the computational cost (3000$\times$ speed-up compared to physical simulation). On simulated image-classification tasks, graph-optimised networks substantially improve classification accuracy. As our framework operates on network topology rather than substrate-specific physics, we anticipate it can transfer to other network-based physical learning systems, providing an efficient route for the directed design and optimisation of complex, strongly-interacting physical neural networks.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

### [84] [Density Estimation on Compact Manifolds under Intrinsic Spectral Block Variation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12637)
*Olga Klopp,Fedor Noskov*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在紧致连通黎曼流形上进行非参数密度估计的内蕴谱稀疏性模型，通过整体处理拉普拉斯-贝尔特米算子的特征空间而非依赖特定基底，实现了基底无关且等距不变的稀疏性建模，并建立了相应的逼近理论与自适应估计方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于拉普拉斯-贝尔特米特征基展开的密度估计方法依赖于特定基的选择，缺乏几何不变性；本文旨在建立一种内在的、不依赖坐标与基底选择的谱稀疏模型，以更好地反映流形的几何结构。

Method: 引入基于完整特征空间分块的希尔伯特范数来度量谱成分的稀疏性，构建块变差空间，并设计坐标无关的块收缩估计器和基于指数谱筛的正则化对数密度估计方法，结合信号依赖的oracle不等式与风险分析。

Result: 建立了块变差空间的结构性质与逼近能力，证明了非渐近L²-oracle不等式与自适应风险界，在多项式谱增长条件下识别出由高维单一块或累积谱复杂度主导的两种误差机制，并给出了匹配的极小极大下界；进一步导出了Kullback-Leibler、Hellinger和L²风险的一致上界。

Conclusion: 所提出的谱稀疏模型具有基底无关性和等距不变性，为流形上的稀疏密度估计提供了尊重几何结构的统一框架，且在理论性能上达到了最优收敛速率。

Abstract: We introduce an intrinsic spectral sparsity model for nonparametric density estimation on compact connected Riemannian manifolds. Instead of penalizing coefficients in an arbitrarily chosen Laplace--Beltrami eigenbasis, we group each complete eigenspace and measure the Hilbert norm of its spectral component. The resulting block-variation space is basis independent and isometry invariant. We establish its structural, atomic, and nonlinear approximation properties and clarify its relation to Sobolev, Besov, and coefficientwise spectral $\ell^1$ classes. We then construct a coordinate-free block-shrinkage estimator and prove a nonasymptotic signal-dependent $L^2$-oracle inequality that adapts to the unknown set of detectable eigenspaces. Under polynomial spectral growth, the risk theory separates the number of spectral blocks from their multiplicities and exhibits two regimes: one driven by a single high-dimensional eigenspace and the other by cumulative spectral complexity. Under matching spectral-growth and nondegeneracy assumptions, corresponding minimax lower bounds show that this multiplicity dependence is intrinsic, with sharp consequences for spheres and the rotation group $SO(3)$. Finally, we develop a positive, normalized, block-penalized exponential spectral sieve for log-densities and derive likelihood oracle inequalities together with expected Kullback--Leibler, Hellinger, and $L^2$ risk bounds. The resulting framework provides a geometry-respecting theory of sparse density estimation that remains invariant under changes of eigenbasis.

</details>


### [85] [Universality of e-detectors for ARL control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12660)
*Aaditya Ramdas*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出了e-detector的概念，用于在变化前的分布类中进行检测，并证明了其不仅能够控制平均运行长度（ARL），还满足可选时间范围不等式，从而避免早期误报。同时，文章指出任何满足该不等式的停止时间必然来自于对e-detector的阈值化，并给出了仅满足ARL控制的弱e-detector的通用表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了更好地控制变化检测中的误报率并提供更灵活的监控机制，研究者希望找到一种既能保证平均运行长度又能适应数据依赖性停止时间的方法。

Method: 通过引入e-detector这一非负过程，结合数学期望和停止时间的分析，推导出其满足可选时间范围不等式，并证明其与阈值化之间的等价关系，进一步提出弱e-detector的概念以涵盖更广泛的检测情形。

Result: 证明了e-detector不仅能控制ARL，还满足可选时间范围不等式；揭示了所有满足该不等式的停止时间都源于e-detector的阈值化；提出了弱e-detector及其对应的通用表示形式。

Conclusion: e-detector为变化检测提供了更强的理论保障，不仅适用于固定时间点的判断，还能应对数据依赖的监控场景，且几乎所有具有ARL控制能力的过程都可以用弱e-detector来表示。

Abstract: An e-detector for a pre-change class $\mathcal P$ is a nonnegative process $M$ such that $\mathbb E_P[M_τ] \leq \mathbb E_P[τ]$ for all stopping times $τ$ and all $P \in \mathcal P$. Thresholding e-detectors controls the average run length (ARL): declaring a change at the first time $T_b$ when $M$ crosses $b$ ensures that $\inf_{P \in \mathcal P}\mathbb E_P[T] \geq b$. But e-detectors do substantially more than control the ARL; they also satisfy a \emph{optional-horizon inequality}: \[ P(T_b\leqσ)\leq \mathbb E_P[σ]/b \] for every data-dependent stopping time (monitoring horizon) \(σ\) and $P\in \mathcal P$. In particular, every e-detector-based procedure obeys $P(T\leq t)\leq t/b$ at each fixed $t$, thus avoiding early false alarms. Remarkably, the converse also holds: every stopping time $T$ that satisfies the optional-horizon inequality must in fact arise from thresholding an e-detector. We also derive a universal representation of stopping times that satisfy (only) ARL control. These are represented by \emph{weak} e-detectors, that only require $\mathbb E_P[M_τ] \leq \mathbb E_P[τ]$ to hold at all threshold stopping times $T_b$. Appendices present universal representations for other (less common) change detection metrics.

</details>


### [86] [Sharp proper estimation of fixed-component Gaussian location mixtures in polynomial time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12701)
*Hengzhi He,Guang Cheng*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文解决了在无分离或最小权重条件下，固定k个单位协方差高斯混合模型的最优Hellinger风险估计问题，提出了基于矩纤维范围查找器的新方法，在多项式时间内实现了最优收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法无法在固定k≥3时以多项式时间达到sharp rate √(d/n)，本文旨在解决这一开放问题。

Method: 提出一种称为矩纤维范围查找器的新技术，利用二阶矩子空间控制投影损失的能量，并估计有限个单自由索引的Hermite收缩向量，从而恢复包含一个缺失方向的张量分量；在仅依赖k的低维空间中进行 exhaustive 矩拟合。

Result: 构造了一个可在多项式时间内运行的适当估计量，达到了最优Hellinger风险率√(d/n) ∧ 1，解决了Doss, Wu, Yang 和 Zhou (2023)遗留的问题。

Conclusion: 对于任意固定的k，该方法首次在无分离条件下实现了高斯混合模型的最优收敛速率，并具有计算可行性。

Abstract: We consider a mixture of at most $k$ unit-covariance Gaussians in $\mathbb{R}^d$ whose means belong to a fixed-radius ball, with no separation or minimum-weight condition. Doss, Wu, Yang and Zhou (2023) proved that the minimax Hellinger risk is of order $\sqrt{d/n}\wedge 1$ and constructed a proper polynomial-time estimator with the slower general bound $(d/n)^{1/4}$; obtaining the sharp rate in polynomial time for fixed $k\geq 3$ was left open. We resolve this question. The key device is a moment-fiber range finder. A second-moment subspace controls the energy missed by projection. We then estimate finitely many one-free-index Hermite contractions. These vector-valued contractions recover every tensor component containing exactly one missed direction at the sharp $\sqrt{d/n}$ scale. Every remaining term contains at least two missed factors and is therefore controlled by the residual second-moment energy. The resulting subspace has dimension depending only on $k$. Exhaustive moment fitting in this constant-dimensional space produces a proper mixture and, together with the dimension-free moment characterization of Gaussian mixtures, achieves the optimal Hellinger rate in polynomial arithmetic time for every fixed $k$.

</details>


### [87] [Hyper-V uniform ergodicity of Markov chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12738)
*Austin Brown,Kshitij Khare*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的均匀漂移条件和局部小化条件，实现了更强的加权一致遍历性（称为超-V 一致遍历性），避免了全局小化条件的复杂验证，并在Polya-Gamma和Kolmogorov-Gamma Gibbs采样器中展示了其应用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统马尔可夫链分析常依赖全局小化条件，难以验证且限制强，本文旨在提出更易验证且提供更强收敛保证的新框架。

Method: 引入新的均匀漂移条件与局部小化条件，定义超-V 一致遍历性，并建立最优极小极大意义下的收敛界。

Result: 证明了无需全局小化即可实现对所有受控制函数V支配的函数的几何偏差衰减，并成功应用于两类Gibbs采样器；还表明可通过不变测度形式直接推断两变量Gibbs采样器的超-V 一致性。

Conclusion: 新框架降低了验证难度，提供了更强的收敛保证，扩展了马尔可夫链收敛性分析的适用范围。

Abstract: We develop a new uniform drift condition and local minorization that implies a stronger weighted form of uniform ergodicity for Markov chains we call hyper-V uniform ergodicity. The convergence guarantees geometric decay of the bias towards the invariant measure independently of the initialization for all functions controlled by a dominating function V. A key advantage of the approach is that it bypasses the need to establish a global minorization condition, which is often substantially more difficult to verify in practice, while yielding stronger convergence guarantees than global minorization. Optimal convergence bounds in a minimax sense of the framework are established. The utility of the framework is demonstrated through applications to the P'olya-Gamma and Kolmogorov-Gamma Gibbs samplers. We also show qualitative hyper-V uniform ergodicity convergence for two-variable Gibbs samplers can be inferred by the form of the invariant measure, bypassing convergence analysis entirely.

</details>


### [88] [Weighted Nuclear Elastic Net Estimation of (Near-) Low-Rank Drift Matrices in Ornstein-Uhlenbeck Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12838)
*Dmytro Marushkevych,Francisco Pina,Mark Podolskij*

Main category: math.ST

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高维连续观测的Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程中，当漂移矩阵为精确或近似低秩时，传统方法因经验协方差矩阵病态而失效，需发展新方法应对非稳定方向和非遍历性带来的挑战。

Method: 引入加权核弹性网络估计器，结合岭正则化与基于经验似然几何的核范数惩罚；在一般可对角化的谱框架下，使用自归一化鞅方法控制随机项，并验证经验曲率条件以获得Frobenius范数误差界。

Result: 建立了相对于任意低秩比较矩阵的oracle不等式；对于近低秩情形，误差由加权后奇异值衰减衡量；在对称半正定精确低秩模型下，估计器的Frobenius误差以高概率达到$rd\log(T)/T$的收敛速率。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效解决了非遍历、病态协方差下的高维低秩漂移估计问题，在适当调参下实现了接近标准矩阵估计的最优缩放律。

Abstract: We study estimation of the drift matrix in a continuously observed high-dimensional Ornstein-Uhlenbeck process when the drift is exactly or approximately low rank. In this setting, exact low rank induces non-stable directions and hence a non-ergodic regime, resulting in a poorly conditioned empirical covariance matrix. To address this difficulty, we introduce a Weighted Nuclear Elastic Net Estimator that combines ridge regularization with a nuclear-norm penalty expressed in the empirical likelihood geometry.
  Under a general diagonalizable spectral framework, we establish oracle inequalities relative to arbitrary low-rank comparison matrices. For near low-rank drifts, the approximation error is naturally measured through the singular-value decay of the drift after weighting by the regularized empirical covariance. The stochastic term is controlled by self-normalized martingale arguments under appropriate choice of the tuning parameter.
  For a symmetric positive-semidefinite exact low-rank model, we verify the empirical-curvature condition required to translate the weighted bound into a Frobenius-norm bound. With an appropriate choice of tuning parameters, the resulting estimator satisfies, up to a logarithmic factor, the standard rank-$r$ matrix-estimation scaling $r d/T$: specifically, its squared Frobenius error is of order $r d\log(T)/T$ with high probability, under an explicit dimension-horizon condition.

</details>

*Patrick Forré*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文介绍了线性独立成分分析（ICA）模型的数学基础，重点研究了在不同假设下独立源的可识别性，并提出了用于从数据中恢复独立源的在线等变梯度下降ICA算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为具备测度论概率背景的读者提供一个自包含的ICA模型理论概述，澄清其数学基础并系统地展示独立源的可识别性条件。

Method: 基于特征函数的解析性质，逐步加强源信号的假设（从非恒定到非高斯再到无高斯独立源），推导ICA模型的可识别性结果；同时介绍在标准无噪声、非高斯ICA设定下的在线等变梯度下降算法。

Result: 在最严格的假设下，独立源在平移、排列、尺度和符号意义下是可识别的，即使存在加性高斯噪声也成立；同时给出了可用于实际估计的在线等变梯度下降ICA算法。

Conclusion: ICA模型在适当的假设下具有良好的理论可识别性，且可通过等变梯度下降方法有效求解，为其在实际应用中的可靠性提供了数学支持。

Abstract: We present the mathematical foundations of linear independent component analysis (ICA) models based on standard literature in a self-contained note. It is aimed at readers with a background in measure-theoretic probability theory. We first develop the theory of the characteristic functions of probability measures on $\mathbb{R}^d$, including their analyticity and the way in which they determine and characterise the distributions. We then focus on several identifiability results of ICA models with successively strengthened assumptions on the sources: from merely non-constant, to non-Gaussian, to Gaussian-free independent sources. Under the strictest assumptions, we show that the independent sources are identifiable up to translation, permutation, scales and signs, and this even in the presence of additive Gaussian noise. Furthermore, we present the online equivariant gradient descent ICA algorithm for recovering the independent sources from data, in the standard complete noiseless non-Gaussian ICA setting.

</details>


### [90] [On Bridging Mixture Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13383)
*Pierre Del Moral,Ajay Jasra,Ke Zhao*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在两种混合概率测度之间构建桥接的方法，利用马尔可夫核生成样本，并证明了其在熵意义下的最优性。针对高斯混合模型和薛定谔桥，建立了基于协方差膨胀的近似桥与真实桥之间的2-Wasserstein连续性界限，结果依赖于扰动Riccati映射的新连续性分析，并在参数估计下给出了误差上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在许多应用中需要在两个混合分布之间进行采样和推断，但通常无法直接获得底层混合结构和转移核；因此，需要一种能够处理估计误差并保持理论保证的桥接方法。

Method: 通过引入参考状态空间和扩展状态空间，结合马尔可夫核构造混合分布间的桥接机制；对于高斯混合使用Schrödinger Bridge框架，并利用ε-协方差膨胀技术进行近似，同时分析扰动Riccati映射的连续性以建立Wasserstein距离误差界。

Result: 证明了所提桥接方法在熵意义上的最优性；建立了近似桥与真实桥之间的2-Wasserstein连续性误差界：对于EM算法估计参数的高斯混合模型，在一定假设下以至少1−10N^−1的概率误差为O([(d log N/N)^1/2 {1+(d log N/N)^1/2 (ε^−2+1)} + ε^2])；对于单高斯及经验估计情形，期望误差为O(d{(1+ε^−2)/(1+N) + ε^2})。

Conclusion: 该方法为混合分布间的采样提供了理论上最优且鲁棒的桥接方案，尤其适用于高斯混合模型；所提出的误差界揭示了样本数、维度与近似精度之间的权衡关系，数值实验验证了理论结果的有效性。

Abstract: In this article we consider bridging between two mixture probability measures. In particular, given access to a Markov kernel between two component distributions, we provide a general mechanism to generate samples from one mixture to the other. Associated to a given reference and extended state space, we prove entropic optimality of this approach. In order to use this idea one needs to know the underlying mixtures and the Markov kernel, which is seldom available, and so we consider the case of Gaussian mixtures and Schrödinger Bridges. We prove a general $2-$Wasserstein continuity bound between the exact bridge and one that is approximated, based on $ε-$covariance inflation, and these rely on a novel continuity analysis of perturbed Riccati maps. We apply our results in the context of bridging mixtures of Gaussians, single Gaussians and empirical estimators of the Gaussian parameters and the Monge map. For mixtures of Gaussians, when the parameters are estimated using the Expectation-Maxmization algorithm, the upper-bound on the $2-$Wasserstein distance between the true and approximated bridges is, under assumptions and with probability at least $1-10N^{-1}$, $\mathcal{O}\big(\big[\big(\tfrac{d\log N}{N}\big)^{1/2}\left\{1+\big(\tfrac{d\log N}{N}\right)^{1/2}(ε^{-2}+1)\big\}+ε^2\big]\big) $
and for the other two cases, in expectation, $\mathcal{O}\left(d\left\{\tfrac{1+ε^{-2}}{1+N}+ε^2\right\}\right)$, where $d,N\in\mathbb{N}$ is the dimension of the Gaussian and the number of empirical samples respectively. We also investigate our bounds numerically.

</details>


### [91] [On the Structural Limits of Machine Learning Decision Systems: An Information-Theoretic, Interaction-Based, and Stochastic-Dynamical Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13510)
*Nestor R. Barraza,Gabriel Pena*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文从信息论和交互建模的角度探讨了数据驱动决策系统的内在性能极限，强调预测能力的根本限制源于数据生成过程的结构特性而非算法本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究机器学习性能的根本限制，揭示算法表现受数据生成模型约束的本质问题。

Method: 利用Fano界和Cramér-Rao不等式分析分类与估计的最小可实现误差，结合马尔可夫随机场等交互模型探讨依赖结构，并将决策系统建模为反馈驱动的随机过程。

Result: 表明预测精度存在由数据结构决定的信息论下限，隐含假设（如独立性、遍历性）显著影响推断有效性，且复杂行为可能从状态依赖动态中涌现。

Conclusion: 提升预测能力的前提是建立对数据生成机制的充分建模，算法优化必须置于模型所施加的信息极限框架内。

Abstract: Machine learning procedures are commonly evaluated in terms of predictive accuracy and computational efficiency. However, their achievable performance is fundamentally constrained by structural properties of the underlying data-generating process, which are formalized in terms of informational bounds. In this work we examine intrinsic limits of data-driven decision systems from an information-theoretic and interaction-based perspective. We analyze minimal achievable error in classification through Fano-type bounds and precision limits in parametric estimation via the Cramér-Rao inequality, emphasizing that such limits depend on the underlying model rather than on algorithmic sophistication alone. We further discuss how implicit assumptions, such as independence, ergodicity, and distributional stability, affect the validity of inferential procedures. Building on interaction-based modeling principles, we review typical frameworks such as Markov Random Fields and potential based representations for encoding dependence mechanisms. We also describe decision systems, including LLM-integrated agent architectures, as feedback-driven stochastic processes where state-dependent dynamics may induce emergent macroscopic behavior. This perspective highlights the importance of having adequate models for the data as a prerequi- site for expanding predictive capability, and situates algorithmic learning within the informational limits imposed by the models.

</details>

### [92] [Identifying changing partial differential equations using Sampled Local WeakIdent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12479)
*Wenbo Hao,Mengyi Tang,Sung Ha Kang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为SLW-Ident的新框架，用于从单一数据集中识别变化的控制方程，结合局部采样与全局信息，有效稳定识别过程并准确恢复具有变系数的偏微分方程及其区域划分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的有限元方法在识别变系数微分方程时存在局限性，且对局部扰动敏感，因此需要一种更稳定、高效且能融合全局信息的局部识别方法。

Method: 提出SLW-Ident框架：首先在整个域内采样局部小块（patches），在每个小块上使用Local WeakIdent识别局部方程；然后利用残差误差检测不同方程间的过渡区域；在每个‘单一方程区域’内，选取最常出现的方程作为代表，并识别该区域内的常系数或变系数PDE；通过不确定性量化理论关联统计误差与采样块数量。

Result: 数值实验表明，SLW-Ident能够准确恢复变系数PDE的方程形式及其空间分布区域，即使在噪声数据下也表现良好，验证了其稳定性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SLW-Ident是一种有效的数据驱动方法，能够在无需先验知识的情况下识别分段变化的控制方程，兼具计算效率与稳定性，适用于复杂系统中多区域PDE的识别任务。

Abstract: We propose Sampled Local WeakIdent (SLW-Ident), a framework for identifying changing governing equations from a single set of given data. Different from a typical approach of using finite element based approximation to represent varying coefficients, this paper explores a local approach in identification of differential equations. First, we present the power of Local WeakIdent which gives good local identification and is also computationally efficient with a small patch size, yet it can be sensitive to local perturbations. We propose SLW-Ident which stabilizes the identification process and also incorporates global information: we first sample patches in the whole given domain, identify equations for each sampled patch, then use residual error of these equations to find the transitions between different equations. We refer to a region where the support of the identified equation does not change to be a region of one equation. Within each region of one equation, we pick the most frequently identified equation as the identified equation, and find constant as well as varying coefficient PDEs within each region of one equation. This is justified by an uncertainty quantification theory that gives the relation between statistical error of dominant support selection and the number of patches. We provide various numerical experiments showing that SLW-Ident accurately recovers the regions of one equation and the governing equations for changing PDEs with varying coefficients even with noisy given data.

</details>


### [93] [BTF-PINN: Enforcing Dirichlet Boundary Conditions Without Boundary Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12823)
*Wenyu Dong,Shuo Zhang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需边界训练的物理信息神经网络BTF-PINN，用于求解齐次Dirichlet边值问题，通过将边界条件嵌入到无边界损失函数中，并证明了其与原问题的等价性及收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法在处理非插值型Dirichlet问题时面临边界处理困难，需要边界惩罚或特殊参数化，限制了效率与精度。

Method: 设计了一种仅依赖内部区域的变分形式，将本质边界条件直接嵌入损失函数，避免了对边界采样和惩罚项的需求，并证明了其数学等价性和收敛性。

Result: 在高维问题、各向异性椭圆方程和不规则几何域上验证了方法的有效性，相比标准带边界惩罚的PINN，BTF-PINN显著提升了边界迹的精度。

Conclusion: BTF-PINN为求解齐次Dirichlet问题提供了一种高效且精确的新框架，无需边界训练即可保证理论一致性与数值性能。

Abstract: The homogeneous Dirichlet boundary value problem captures the core difficulty of solving Dirichlet problems with non-interpolatory methods. We propose BTF-PINN (Boundary-Training-Free Physics-Informed Neural Network), an interior-only strategy for solving homogeneous Dirichlet boundary value problems that requires no boundary training, boundary penalties, or boundary-conforming parametrizations. The key idea is to embed the essential boundary condition into a newly designed boundary-free loss function. We prove the equivalence between the proposed interior-only variational formulation and the original boundary value problem, establish a sharp threshold condition for the residual weight, and develop a convergence analysis based on the coercivity of the functional. Numerical experiments on high-dimensional problems, irregular geometries, and anisotropic elliptic equations demonstrate the effectiveness of BTF-PINN. Comparisons with standard boundary-penalty PINNs further show that BTF-PINN achieves superior boundary trace accuracy.

</details>

Motivation: 为了分析随机Cahn-Hilliard-Cook方程的数值逼近，特别是其线性化形式在随机扰动下的收敛行为。

Result: 在对Wiener过程协方差算子的适当假设下，证明了强收敛估计，并给出了确定性Cahn-Hilliard方程的详细误差界。

Abstract: The linearized Cahn-Hilliard-Cook equation is
  discretized in the spatial variables by a standard finite element
  method. Strong convergence estimates are proved under suitable
  assumptions on the covariance operator of the Wiener process,
  which is driving the equation. The backward Euler time stepping is
  also studied. The analysis is set in a framework based on analytic
  semigroups. The main effort is spent on proving detailed error
  bounds for the corresponding deterministic Cahn-Hilliard
  equation. The results should be interpreted as results on
  approximation of the stochastic convolution, which is a part of
  the mild solution of the nonlinear Cahn-Hilliard-Cook equation.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决含有小参数ι的梯度弹性Kirchhoff板模型在数值计算中的稳定性与收敛性问题，提出一种无需稳定项的稳健数值方法。

Method: 采用H^3三次有限元构建离散空间，并引入插值映射将低阶项和右端项映射到H^2非协调有限元空间，从而构造无稳定项的非协调有限元格式。

Result: 该方法对参数ι具有鲁棒性，在网格尺寸h下具有一阶一致收敛性，且理论分析得到了数值实验的验证。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效解决了GEKP模型的数值挑战，具备良好的收敛性和参数鲁棒性，无需引入额外的稳定项。

Abstract: In this paper we propose a robust nonconforming finite method for a gradient-elastic Kirchhoff plate (GEKP) model with a small parameter $0<ι<1$. While the method employs an $H^3$ cubic finite element for the discrete space, it employs an interpolation mapping into an $H^2$ nonconforming finite element space in both the lower-order terms of the bilinear form and the right-hand side. Notably, the numerical method is robust with respect to the parameter $ι$ and uniformly convergent to order $h$ without any stabilized terms. Finally, some numerical experiments are performed to verify the theoretical findings.

</details>


### [96] [A Counterexample to Robust Second-Order Convergence of the Strang Projector-Splitting Integrator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12893)
*Shiheng Zhang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 经典Strang投影分裂方法在低秩近似中保留的最小奇异值趋于零时，尽管数值上常观察到二阶收敛，但误差分析仅能证明一阶收敛。本文通过构造反例说明这一差距是固有的，揭示了因子方向快速旋转导致对称组合的误差抵消失效，从而无法获得与奇异值无关的全局二阶收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释为何Strang投影分裂法在奇异值趋近于零时的误差分析只能保证一阶收敛，尽管数值实验常显示二阶收敛，试图填补理论与实践之间的差距。

Method: 构造满足光滑性和有界性条件的3×3矩阵微分方程，并设计具有周期性强迫的四步Strang循环，分析其在一个周期内的局部误差结构，特别关注小奇异值对应因子方向的旋转效应。

Result: 发现精确子流Strang方法在单个周期内存在非零的h²局部误差项，重复该周期可排除与保留奇异值无关的全局二阶误差界；揭示了投影空间变化阻碍误差抵消的机制。

Conclusion: 在标准假设下，经典投影分裂Strang方法的一阶收敛性结果无法提升为鲁棒的二阶收敛，该收敛阶差距是内在的。

Abstract: The classical Strang projector-splitting integrator is widely observed to converge with order two, whereas the error analysis that remains uniform as the smallest singular value retained in the low-rank approximation tends to zero proves only order one. We show that this gap is intrinsic under the standard assumptions. We construct $3\times3$ matrix differential equations that are $C^2$ in time and smooth in the matrix variable, with rank-two initial data that satisfy uniform boundedness, Lipschitz, tangency-defect, and regularity bounds. Nevertheless, the exact-subflow Strang method has a nonzero $h^2$ term in the local error over one periodic forcing cycle consisting of four Strang steps. Repetition of that cycle rules out a global second-order bound whose constant and stepsize threshold are independent of the retained singular values. The mechanism is a rapid rotation of the factor directions associated with the small singular value: first-order consistency is preserved, but the changing projection spaces prevent the cancellation normally expected from a symmetric Strang composition. Hence the robust first-order result cannot, under these assumptions alone, be upgraded to robust second order for the classical projector-splitting method.

</details>


### [97] [Analysis of Error Propagation in Autoencoder-Based Reduced-Order Neural Ordinary Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13132)
*Jingyi Zhang,Gwanghyun Jo*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于路径积分恒等式的分析方法，用于研究神经网络ODE降阶模型在长时程外推中的误差传播机制，并通过Jacobian范数量化潜空间动力学的传输敏感性，在Burgers和Gray-Scott系统中发现了两种不同的误差演化模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经ODE降阶模型在局部预测精度上表现良好，但在长时程外推中行为差异显著，亟需理论工具揭示其误差来源与传播机制。

Method: 提出路径积分恒等式以分离潜空间动力学中的局部误差注入与放大过程，并引入多步Jacobian范数作为传输敏感性度量指标。

Result: 在Burgers系统中误差保持有界且主要源于持续的局部偏差；而在Gray-Scott系统中则表现出显著的误差放大现象，Jacobian范数可作为敏感性诊断工具而非直接的物理预测精度指标。

Conclusion: 该分析框架有效区分了不同类型的误差传播机制，揭示了Jacobian范数在复杂系统长时程预测中的诊断价值。

Abstract: Neural ODE reduced-order models often achieve comparable local prediction accuracy, yet their long-horizon extrapolation behavior can differ substantially. To analyze this discrepancy, we develop a path-integral identity that separates local discrepancy injection from amplification in the learned latent dynamics. The associated multi-step Jacobian norms quantify transport sensitivity and distinguish different propagation regimes. Experiments on the Burgers and Gray--Scott systems exhibit two distinct patterns of error evolution. In Burgers systems, prediction errors remain bounded and are primarily associated with persistent local discrepancies. In contrast, Gray--Scott systems exhibit pronounced amplification during extrapolation, where Jacobian norms serve as sensitivity diagnostics rather than direct indicators of physical prediction accuracy.

</details>


### [98] [Performance Evaluation of an Adaptive Quadrature and a Double Exponential Formula Using Arbitrary-Precision Floating-Point Arithmetic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13187)
*Tomonori Kouya*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文实现了AQE11D和双指数（DE）公式，并在Kahaner的21个测试问题上进行评估，结果表明AQE11D在所有问题上均达到目标精度，而DE公式在处理端点奇异性时表现优异但对区间内奇异性问题失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了高精度数值积分，研究不同自适应积分方法在极端精度要求下的性能差异，特别是面对奇异性问题时的表现。

Method: 使用GNU多重精度浮点可靠库提供的任意精度算术，实现Ninomiya的自适应9点Newton-Cotes规则扩展版本（AQE11D）以及Takahasi和Moris的双指数公式，并在Kahaner的21个测试问题上以绝对容差10^{-50}和10^{-100}进行评估。

Result: AQE11D在所有21个问题上均达到目标精度；但在强端点奇异性情况下（如1/√x），在10^{-100}容差下需要约5.4×10^7次函数求值，约为DE公式的7×10^4倍；DE公式在两种容差下收敛于18个问题，显示出对端点奇异性较强的鲁棒性，但对区间内部奇异性问题失败。

Conclusion: AQE11D具有更广的适用性，而DE公式在处理端点奇异性时效率显著更高，但无法处理区间内奇异性，需进一步改进。

Abstract: Using arbitrary-precision arithmetic provided by the GNU Multiple Precision Floating-Point Reliable Library, we implement AQE11D---that is, Ninomiya's adaptive 9-point Newton--Cotes rule extended with a sequence of higher-order rules---and Takahasi and Moris' double exponential (DE) formula. We evaluate them for Kahaner's 21 test problems. For both absolute tolerances $10^{-50}$ and $10^{-100}$, AQE11D attains target accuracy on all 21 problems; however, for strong endpoint singularity such as $1/\sqrt{x}$, it requires about $5.4\times10^{7}$ function evaluations at $10^{-100}$, roughly $7\times10^{4}$ times as many as the DE formula. The formula converges on 18 problems at both tolerances, demonstrating its strength against endpoint singularities but also its failure, as it stands, on problems with a singularity inside the integration interval.

</details>


### [99] [Stochastic resistive Hall--MHD with current fluctuations: martingale weak solutions via a convergent structure-preserving finite element method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13407)
*Agus L. Soenjaya*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种全离散、线性隐式、保持结构的混合有限元方法，用于求解具有旋度型噪声的随机电阻Hall-MHD系统，并证明了其收敛性和存在性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在有界凸多面域上研究受非线性完全导电边界条件约束的随机电阻Hall-MHD系统，需要发展能够保持物理结构（如磁高斯定律）的数值方法。

Method: 基于相容离散de Rham复形，开发了一种全离散、线性隐式的混合有限元方法，该方法能精确保持磁高斯定律，并处理动量方程中的乘法高斯噪声和感应方程中的旋度型噪声。

Result: 证明了离散解的子序列收敛到有限能量弱鞅解，从而为带有旋度型噪声的随机Hall-MHD系统提供了构造性的存在性证明；数值实验验证了方法的随机动力学行为、精确的散度保持性和收敛性。

Conclusion: 所提出的数值方法有效且可靠，不仅保持了系统的物理结构，还为随机Hall-MHD系统的理论分析提供了支持。

Abstract: We study the stochastic resistive Hall--magnetohydrodynamic (Hall--MHD) system on bounded convex polyhedral domains, subject to nonlinear perfectly conducting boundary conditions. The system is driven by multiplicative Gaussian forcing in the momentum equation together with structured curl-type noise in the induction equation arising from stochastic perturbations of the resistive and Hall parts in the generalised Ohm law. We develop a fully discrete, linearly implicit, structure-preserving mixed finite element method based on a compatible discrete de Rham complex, which preserves the magnetic Gauss law exactly. We prove subsequential convergence of the discrete solutions to a finite-energy weak martingale solution, thereby obtaining a constructive existence result for the stochastic Hall--MHD system with curl-type noise. Numerical experiments illustrate the stochastic dynamics, exact divergence preservation, and convergence of the method.

</details>

### [100] [Dimension Reduction of Higher-Order Dynamical Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12676)
*Amitosh Tiwari,Chittaranjan Hens,Prosenjit Kundu*

Main category: nlin.CD

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对仅包含高阶相互作用的复杂网络动力系统的单维降维方法，通过定义有效的三角相互作用强度（β_Δ）来实现，并在合成和真实网络上验证了该模型能够准确捕捉系统的稳态行为和状态转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于大多数现有的降维方法局限于成对相互作用，难以适用于具有高阶相互作用的复杂网络，因此需要发展一种适用于纯高阶相互作用网络的动力系统降维框架。

Method: 提出基于有效高阶相互作用强度（β_Δ）的一维降维理论框架，该强度与网络中的三角形结构及系统的有效状态相关，并在三种仅有高阶相互作用的动力学模型中进行理论推导与数值验证。

Result: 降维精度主要取决于节点状态的同质性；当节点状态差异较小时，降维效果更优；在合成与真实网络上的实验表明，该简化模型能高精度地复现原系统的有效稳态和相变行为。

Conclusion: 所提出的降维方法成功扩展了传统框架，适用于具有纯高阶相互作用的网络动力系统，为分析高维复杂系统提供了简洁而有效的工具。

Abstract: Low-dimensional reductions provide a useful framework for studying high-dimensional dynamics on complex networks, but most existing approaches are restricted to pairwise interactions. Here, we develop a one-dimensional reduction for dynamical systems on networks with purely higher-order interactions. The reduction is formulated through an effective higher-order interaction strength ($β_Δ$), associated with the triangular interactions of the underlying network and the dynamical system's effective state. We present a theoretical framework for the dimension-reduction approach and validate it across three dynamical models with exclusively higher-order interactions. We find that the reduction accuracy is mainly determined by the homogeneity of node states, i.e., the deviations in state values become very small. Numerical results on synthetic and real networks show that the reduced model captures the effective steady states and transitions of the full system with good accuracy.

</details>

### [101] [Excitation-Supervised Closed-Loop Self-Calibration and Target Seeking for an Unknown-Pose Range-Bearing Relay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12528)
*Yash Bagla*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种闭环控制方法，利用轨迹扩散裕度 $S_v$ 监督车辆在寻找隐藏目标时的激励-重置控制器，确保中继校准的可靠性，并在有限时间内实现精确估计和目标追踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车辆在通过未知位置和偏航角的中继进行目标搜索时，需要在线判断中继校准是否可信，并在校准不足时采取相应行动。现有静态判据无法实时指导运动决策，因此需要一种动态闭环机制来保证校准质量。

Method: 引入轨迹扩散裕度 $S_v$ 作为可识别性的指标，将其用于监督激励-重置控制器；当扩散证书不足时重新触发探索性运动，并将目标搜索输入从激励方向投影分离，从而实现动态校准与目标搜索的协调。

Result: 在显式采样假设下，该方法能在有限时间内获得所需激励；在无噪声局部区域，估计器收敛后可实现目标搜索的收敛；仿真和实验验证了方法的有效性，在100次配对试验中保持100%成功率，而固定调度方法的成功率下降至56%。

Conclusion: 轨迹扩散裕度 $S_v$ 可有效作为校准准确性的监督信号，所提出的闭环激励监督算法优于固定调度方法，能够在不确定环境中自适应地平衡探索与利用，保障高精度的目标跟踪和中继校准。

Abstract: A vehicle seeking a hidden target through a range-bearing relay of unknown position and yaw must decide, online, whether its own motion has already made the relay calibration trustworthy, and what to do when it has not. Two distinct vehicle-relative observations are known to remove the calibration gauge and make the target's relay-local packet globally actionable (arXiv:2608.09464), but that statement is static: it classifies a stored window only after the fact. This paper supplies the closed-loop layer: we show that the trajectory-spread margin $S_v$ that governs identifiability is simultaneously a finite-noise seed-accuracy bound, a local-vector variance decomposition, and a circle-geometry excitation budget, and we use it to supervise an excitation-reset controller. An excitation-supervised algorithm retriggers exploratory motion whenever the spread certificate is insufficient, projecting the target-seeking input away from the excitation's push, and otherwise proceeds to unrestricted target seeking. Under explicit sampling assumptions the supervision rule provably acquires any required excitation in finite time; in the noiseless local regime with positive excitation decay, estimator convergence yields target-seeking convergence after certification; and the threshold is selected from a desired calibration-accuracy level rather than chosen heuristically. Closed-loop simulation, paired Monte Carlo comparisons, a spread-threshold ablation, and a ROS 2/Gazebo software-in-the-loop experiment with sensing delay validate the approach. A decay-rate sweep shows that supervision matters when a fixed schedule's decay outruns the unknown time-to-adequate-excitation: over 100 paired trials the fixed baseline's yaw RMSE rises from 0.010 to 0.065 rad and success falls to 56%, while target-tracking error remains insensitive; supervision keeps yaw RMSE between 0.0095 and 0.0191 rad with 100% success.

</details>


### [102] [The $θ$-Symmetric SRG with Applications to Stability of Cactus Dynamic Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12591)
*Xiaokan Yang,Wei Chen,Li Qiu*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种改进的缩放相对图（SRG）变体——θ-对称SRG，用于分析cactus网络的稳定性，能够刻画相位超前与滞后行为，并通过半定规划计算θ-分段相位，建立了循环和并联反馈结构的统一稳定性框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SRG在描述多变量系统相位特性方面存在局限，难以自然推广经典Nyquist图的几何直观性，且对复杂反馈结构（如cactus网络）的稳定性分析保守性强，需更灵活、直观的分析工具。

Method: 引入θ-对称SRG，分别分析其增益与相位特性，建立θ-分段相位与范数最小化问题的联系，并利用半定规划进行计算；推导其乘法次可积性和加法次可加性，结合循环与并联反馈结构，构建多环cactus网络的统一稳定性判据。

Result: 实现了对cactus网络中循环和并联反馈结构的精确稳定性分析，给出了循环互连鲁棒稳定性的充要条件，形成了适用于一般多环cactus网络的统一稳定性框架，并可通过半定规划有效计算关键参数。

Conclusion: θ-对称SRG为多变量反馈系统的稳定性分析提供了更自然、直观且保守性更低的几何工具，所提出的框架有效拓展了传统Nyquist方法的应用范围，特别适用于复杂结构如cactus网络的分析。

Abstract: In this paper, we systematically study a variant of the scaled relative graph (SRG), referred to as the $θ$-symmetric SRG, and apply it to the stability analysis of cactus networks. Compared with the previous SRG definition, the $θ$-symmetric SRG enables the characterization of phase lead and lag behaviors, and serves as a more natural multivariable extension of the classical Nyquist plot. We first analyze the gain and phase aspects of $θ$-symmetric SRG separately and build a connection between $θ$-segmental phase and a norm minimization problem. This connection makes it possible to compute $θ$-segmental phase via semidefinite programming. We further derive the submultiplicative and subadditive properties of $θ$-symmetric SRG. These algebraic properties are crucial to determine the nonsingularity of product-type and sum-type return difference matrices, which topologically correspond to the cyclic and parallel-feedback extreme cases of cactus networks. By taking the cyclic interconnection as the fundamental starting point, we establish necessary and sufficient conditions for its robust stability. Integrating this with the parallel case, we synthesize a unified stability framework for general multi-loop cactus networks. The $θ$-symmetric SRG framework is less conservative and provides a more intuitive geometric interpretation of system behaviors compared with some existing approaches. Several examples are included to demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed methods.

</details>


### [103] [Security-Constrained Operation of IBR-Dominated Power Systems: Static and Dynamic Security Across Preventive and Corrective Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12609)
*Buxin She*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种两轴视角来分析基于逆变器的资源（IBR）对电力系统安全运行的影响，涵盖静态与动态安全、预防性与校正性决策时机，并综述了现有模型与方法，指出IBR能力可扩展可行域或缓解安全约束，但其实际可交付性取决于共用的能力与约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着IBR在电力系统中的广泛应用，其快速动态特性和可控性正在改变传统安全约束运行的建模与协调方式，亟需统一框架以系统化理解其影响。

Method: 提出静态vs动态安全、预防vs校正决策的两轴分类框架，建立通用数学模型以显式表达预防-校正权衡，并对现有研究进行综述与整合。

Result: 1）IBR能力可扩大可行域或缓解安全约束，降低运行成本或提升安全性；2）不同模型中共有的能力和约束决定了该能力是否可在实际运行中兑现。

Conclusion: 未来研究应聚焦于IBR能力的精确表征与量化、联合调度机制设计以及可扩展的求解框架。

Abstract: Inverter-based resources (IBRs) couple power system operation to fast dynamics and controller-dependent responses. Their configurable capabilities are reshaping the formulation and coordination of security-constrained operation. This paper presents a two-axis view: static versus dynamic security and preventive versus corrective decision timing. Preventive scheduling is extending from static post-contingency feasibility toward dynamic security, while corrective operation spans equilibrium-based corrective actions and trajectory-based control. A generic formulation represents this change and makes the preventive--corrective tradeoff explicit. Existing formulations, methods, and capability representations are reviewed and synthesized within this framework. The surveyed work yields two findings. First, IBR capability can expand the feasible set or relieve security constraints, reducing operating cost or improving security performance. Second, shared capability and constraints across formulations determine whether that capability remains operationally deliverable. These findings motivate future research in IBR capability characterization and quantification, joint scheduling, and scalable solution frameworks.

</details>


### [104] [Improving Fast Charging Safety With Core Temperature Estimation Via Kolmogorov-Arnold Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12638)
*Faysal Ahamed,Tanushree Roy*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合Kolmogorov-Arnold网络与鲁棒控制屏障函数的电池快速充电框架，利用表面温度等可测变量估计核心温度并确保热安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于锂电池快充时核心温度无法直接测量，导致热安全难以实时保障，需发展可靠的安全控制策略。

Method: 采用Kolmogorov-Arnold网络估计电池核心温度，并将其嵌入鲁棒控制屏障函数（KAN-rCBF）中，结合表面温度、冷却液温度、冷却功率和充电电流等测量值，通过二次规划实现实时安全控制。

Result: 仿真结果表明，该方法在保证电池热安全的同时，充电速度与现有先进方法相当，且相较之下提供了更可靠的热安全性保障。

Conclusion: 所提出的KAN-rCBF框架能在存在估计误差和模型不确定性的情况下，提供分析意义上的安全保证，为锂电池快速充电中的热安全问题提供有效解决方案。

Abstract: Fast charging of Lithium-ion batteries can lead to a significant temperature rise, which can cause serious risks to battery safety and lifetime. To ensure safe battery operation, thermal constraints must be enforced during the fast charging process. However, the core temperature of the battery cannot be directly measured in practice, which makes real-time safety enforcement challenging. This paper proposes a framework that incorporates core temperature estimates from Kolmogorov-Arnold Network within robust control barrier function (KAN-rCBF) constraints for battery fast-charging. The algorithm utilizes measurements from battery surface temperature, coolant temperature, coolant power, and charging current to solve a quadratic programming problem under safety constraints. We prescribe analytical safety guarantees for this optimal charging policy under KAN estimation errors and model uncertainty. Simulation results show that the proposed method maintains a safe battery temperature while achieving charging times comparable to the state-of-the-art method, where the latter fails to guarantee the same level of thermal safety.

</details>


### [105] [Time Distribution Principle Using Measured Traveling Waves in Power Grid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12730)
*Haozong Wang,Yayu Yang,Jiahui Yang,Muhamman Umar Afzaal,Yuru Wu,Yan Wen,Zhengfa Zhang,Yilu Liu*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于电力系统行波测量的新型时间同步方法，利用正向和反向行波传播的对称性实现高精度时间分发，无需依赖外部时间参考。仿真结果表明该方法在正常条件下可达到微秒级同步精度，有望作为传统方法的安全替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统时间同步方法（如卫星和通信-based）存在信号易受干扰、依赖通信延迟对称性等问题，限制了其在分布式系统中的可靠性与安全性，因此需要一种更安全、独立于外部参考的时间同步方案。

Method: 提出基于电力网中行波（TW）测量的时间同步原理，利用前向与反向行波传播的固有对称性进行时间比对，并通过电磁暂态仿真在改进的IEEE 14节点系统上进行验证。

Result: 在电磁暂态仿真中，该方法在正常运行条件下实现了微秒级的时间同步精度，表现出对通信质量和外部时间源的低依赖性。

Conclusion: 基于行波的时间同步方法是一种可行且具有潜力的替代方案，能够提高时间同步的安全性和鲁棒性，适用于对可靠性要求较高的分布式电力系统应用。

Abstract: Accurate time synchronization is essential for distributed systems. Conventional methods, such as satellite-based synchronization and communication-based approaches, face challenges including signal vulnerability and dependence on communication delay symmetry. This paper proposes a novel time synchronization principle based on traveling wave (TW) measurements in power grids. By leveraging the inherent symmetry of forward and backward TW propagation, the proposed method achieves high-precision time distribution without relying on external time references. The methodology is validated through electromagnetic transient simulations on a modified IEEE 14-bus system. The results demonstrate that under normal conditions, the proposed approach achieves microsecond-level synchronization accuracy. These findings suggest that the TW-based time synchronization principle is a potential alternative to traditional methods, offering improved security and reduced dependence on communication quality.

</details>


### [106] [Technical Report on Resilient and Secure Large-Scale Energy Internet Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12916)
*Ioannis Zografopoulos,Karen Largman,Isaac Ortega Romero,S M Zia Ur Rashid,Yexiang Chen,George Fragkos,Charalambos Konstantinou,Subhash Lakshminarayana,Juan Ospina,Airin Rahman,Suman Rath,Vivek Kumar Singh,Mucun Sun,Wei Sun*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本报告探讨了大规模能源互联网（EI）系统的安全与韧性，分析了其网络-物理威胁格局，并提出了相应的检测、保障和缓解技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着能源、信息和市场的深度融合，大规模能源互联网系统面临日益复杂的网络-物理安全挑战，亟需系统性研究其安全性与韧性。

Method: 通过调研现有技术，建立包含储能集成、多维韧性及电价预测的建模、控制与决策框架，引入基于图的抗攻击信息路由方法，并评估人工智能在对抗风险下的可信部署。

Result: 提出了一系列应对EI系统安全威胁的技术与框架，识别了关键脆弱点并展示了提升系统韧性的潜在路径。

Conclusion: 实现安全、韧性的大规模能源互联网需要跨学科的研究推进，以及标准制定和监管政策的协同支持。

Abstract: This IEEE PES Task Force report examines the security and resilience of large-scale Energy Internet (EI) systems, in which electricity, information, and market layers are tightly coupled through pervasive digitalization. The report characterizes the EI cyber-physical threat landscape and surveys detection, assurance, and mitigation techniques, presents modeling, control, and decision-making frameworks that capture cyber-physical interdependencies, including storage integration, multi-dimensional resilience, and electricity price forecasting, examines adversarial risks and trustworthy deployment of artificial intelligence, and introduces graph-based, attack-resilient information routing. The report closes with recommendations for research, standardization, and regulatory efforts needed to realize a resilient and secure large-scale EI.

</details>


### [107] [AoI-Guaranteed Dynamic Route Planning for Connected Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13083)
*Sajedeh Norouzi,Maryam Ansarifard,Farshad Zeinali,Ali Nouruzi,Nader Mokari,Hamid Saeedi,Nizar Zorba*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合行驶时间和信息年龄（AoI）的双因素动态路径规划方法AGDRP，利用深度强化学习优化车联网环境下的路径选择，仿真结果表明该方法在性能上优于仅考虑行驶时间的传统方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的智能交通系统路径规划主要关注行驶时间或道路容量，忽略了车载通信中信息新鲜度的重要性。随着车联网发展，通信质量对交通安全和效率的影响日益显著，因此需要将信息年龄（AoI）纳入路径规划考量。

Method: 提出一种名为AoI-Guaranteed Dynamic Route Planning (AGDRP) 的方法，采用深度强化学习（DRL）框架，同时优化行驶时间和信息年龄（AoI），实现自适应的学习与路径决策。

Result: 仿真结果显示，AGDRP在平衡行驶时间和信息新鲜度方面优于仅以最短行驶时间为优化目标的基线方案，证明了引入AoI最小化可显著提升路径规划的整体性能。

Conclusion: 将信息年龄（AoI）与传统行驶时间共同作为优化目标，能够有效提升车联网环境下动态路径规划的性能，验证了通信感知路径规划在智能交通系统中的潜力。

Abstract: The advancement of Intelligent Transportation Sys- tems (ITS) has been significantly driven by progress in radio communication technology. Dynamic route planning, a key com- ponent of ITS, traditionally focuses on metrics such as route capacity and travel time. This paper presents a novel dual- factor approach that integrates travel time estimation and radio resource availability into an innovative route-planning scheme for connected vehicles (CVs). To address this dual-objective route planning challenge, we employ Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL). Our approach, called AoI-Guaranteed Dynamic Route Planning (AGDRP), effectively balances travel time and Age of Information (AoI), enhancing route planning performance through adaptive learning over time. Simulation results demon- strate that AGDRP outperforms the baseline scheme, which solely focuses on travel time optimization. In fact, we show that incor- porating AoI minimization significantly enhances route planning performance beyond conventional travel-time-based approaches.

</details>


### [108] [Robust Space-Filling Input Design via Stochastic Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13360)
*Máté Kiss,Roland Tóth,Maarten Schoukens*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出了一种鲁棒的空间填充输入设计算法，通过在模型类的总体上优化准则的期望，提高了信号设计对模型不确定性的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统空间填充输入设计依赖于假设模型，当实际系统与假设模型差异较大时性能下降，因此需要一种对模型不确定性更鲁棒的设计方法。

Method: 提出的方法通过对模型类中优化准则的期望进行建模，并采用随机近似技术来优化这一鲁棒性准则，从而生成适用于整个模型类的空间填充数据集。

Result: 仿真示例表明所提算法能够有效生成鲁棒的输入信号，相较于传统方法在模型失配情况下表现更优。

Conclusion: 该鲁棒空间填充输入设计算法能够在模型不确定性存在的情况下提升输入信号的质量和适用性，具有良好的应用潜力。

Abstract: The space-filling input design approach generates a so-called space-filling dataset in the feature space of the system model. The design method is applicable on a broad class of model structures with wide selection of signals and also incorporates information measures through optimality criteria into the signal design. However, during the signal design, knowledge of a hypothesized model is required. The designed signal can perform far from the optimal if the true system is significantly different from the hypothesized system model. This paper proposes a robust space-filling input design algorithm that can generate a space-filling dataset for an entire class of models. The proposed algorithm takes the expectation of an optimality criterion over the population of the model class, and a stochastic approximation technique is employed to optimize this robust criteria. The efficiency of the proposed algorithm is demonstrated in a simulation example.

</details>


### [109] [Process-Aware Cross-Layer Adaptation for O-RAN-Enabled Industrial Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13372)
*Elahe Delavari,Junaid Farooq,M. Majid Butt,Quanyan Zhu*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向闭环工业应用的Process-Aware Co-adaptation Engine框架，通过联合优化无线网络、边缘计算与感知配置，提升系统整体效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的单独评估吞吐量、延迟和推理精度无法反映闭环工业应用是否完成有效任务，需要一种结合物理过程状态与系统各环节的协同优化方法。

Method: 提出了Process-Aware Co-adaptation Engine框架，融合应用结果、物理过程状态、无线遥测、边缘计算状态和感知配置，协同选择整个闭环系统的运行点，并在集成数字孪生、5G O-RAN网络和边缘视觉推理的工厂检测案例中进行评估。

Result: 实验表明，随着生产速度变化，最优资源分配也会变化；独立调整单个组件效率低下；且可通过较少的全系统评估即可找到高效配置。

Conclusion: 协同跨层优化能显著提升闭环工业无线系统的有效性，所提框架可在动态环境中高效找到适合当前工况的系统配置。

Abstract: Wireless networks increasingly support closed-loop industrial applications in which sensed data must be transmitted, processed, and converted into an action before physical process makes the result obsolete. Throughput, latency, and inference accuracy measured separately cannot determine whether such an application completed a useful task. We propose a Process-Aware Co-adaptation Engine framework that combines application outcomes, process state, radio telemetry, edge-compute state, and sensing configuration to select coordinated operating points across the complete loop. We evaluate the proposed approach in a factory-inspection case study that integrates a physics-based digital twin, a programmable 5G O-RAN network, and edge-based visual inference. The experiments show that the preferred resource allocation changes with production speed and that adapting individual system components independently can be inefficient. We further show that efficient configurations can be identified with relatively few full-system evaluations.

</details>


### [110] [AaLLM: An End-to-End Analog Circuit Design Framework from Topology Generation to Sizing Using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13472)
*Mohammed Ayman Habib,Rylan Hart,Morteza Fayazi*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出AaLLM，一个开源的端到端多智能体LLM工作流，用于模拟电路设计，能根据用户需求自动生成包含拓扑结构和器件尺寸的网表，显著减少SPICE调用次数和设计时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于大语言模型的电路设计方法碎片化，依赖人工知识输入，难以兼顾多种设计指标，且无法生成创新拓扑结构，导致设计效率低、易出现幻觉。

Method: 构建一个多智能体系统，包括自动构建知识库的RAG模型，以及由设计师、批评家和评估者组成的三智能体反馈系统，实现拓扑生成与尺寸优化的协同。

Result: AaLLM在多个电路测试中实现了3-4.5倍的SPICE调用减少和40倍的时钟时间缩短，生成的新拓扑结构FoM与已知结构相当甚至高出3倍。

Conclusion: AaLLM通过多智能体协作和知识增强推理，实现了高效、创新的模拟电路自动化设计，显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Analog circuit design is a time-consuming, iterative process in a nonlinear and high-dimensional design space that relies heavily on expert intuition. Among recent developments, LLMs have introduced a promising approach by bringing natural language reasoning to circuit design tasks. The majority of conventional LLM-based approaches provide fragmented solutions that focus either only on sizing or topology generation. These methods require adding specific technical knowledge manually, which is inefficient and prone to hallucinations during circuit sizing. Moreover, the inherent trade-off in meeting different specs makes current approaches iterative and tedious. Another shortcoming is the inability to create innovative topologies, which may lead to sub-optimal designs due to reliance on conventional topologies. In this paper, we present AaLLM, an open-source end-to-end multi-agent LLM workflow that takes user specs as input and outputs the appropriate netlist, encompassing both topology generation and circuit sizing. AaLLM automates the creation of a relevant knowledge base from research papers and textbooks to combat tedious manual data collection. A RAG model is implemented to emulate circuit design expertise using this knowledge base. Moreover, AaLLM uses a novel tri-agent feedback system comprising a Designer that determines circuit component values, a Critic that scrutinizes these values, and an Evaluator that minimizes circuit sizing iterations by arbitrating between the other two agents. AaLLM-generated novel topologies achieve a figure of merit (FoM) comparable to that of known topologies, and up to 3x higher for certain circuits. Testing on several circuit topologies, our results show a 3x - 4.5x decrease in the number of SPICE calls at inference when compared to SOTA multi-agent LLM pipelines. The results also show a 40x decrease in wall-clock time compared to existing approaches.

</details>


### [111] [Safety-Critical Control for Quadrotor UAVs via Decentralized Navigation Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13507)
*Omayra Yago Nieto,Alexandre Anahory Simoes,Leonardo Colombo*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 研究了在模型不确定性下，基于去中心化导航函数的多旋翼无人机群的安全关键控制，提出了一种推力-姿态实现方法，并通过鲁棒高阶控制障碍函数-二次规划安全滤波器确保高概率下的避碰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在存在模型不确定性的情况下实现多旋翼无人机群的安全协同飞行，需要解决欠驱动系统对全驱动参考动力学的跟踪误差与碰撞规避问题。

Method: 采用去中心化导航函数生成全驱动平移参考力，设计推力-姿态实现方式来逼近该参考力，并量化其跟踪误差；利用聚合的鲁棒高阶控制障碍函数-二次规划（HOCBF-QP）安全滤波器对名义推力进行最小修正以保证成对避障。

Result: 所提方法能够有效缩小推力-姿态实现与参考动力学之间的误差，并在高概率下实现无人机间的碰撞避免。

Conclusion: 该框架为存在模型不确定性的多旋翼无人机群提供了安全、去中心化的控制方案，具备实际应用潜力。

Abstract: We study safety-critical control for teams of quadrotor UAVs driven by decentralized navigation functions under learned model uncertainty. These functions generate fully actuated translational reference forces, while quadrotors can only produce thrust along their body-fixed vertical axes. We construct a thrust-attitude implementation of the induced navigation forces and quantify its error with respect to the fully actuated reference dynamics. An aggregated robust HOCBF-QP safety filter minimally modifies the nominal thrusts while guaranteeing pairwise collision avoidance with high probability.

</details>


### [112] [Joint Communication-Control Strategy Optimization with Partially Nested Information Structures: The Linear-Quadratic Case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13535)
*Haoyi You,Kaiqing Zhang*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究了多智能体线性系统中基于共同信息框架的联合通信-控制策略优化（JCCO）问题，针对部分嵌套信息结构提出动态规划方法求解最优控制策略，并推导出闭式Riccati方程，同时扩展至闭环通信策略情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多智能体系统中，通信与控制策略的联合优化面临信息结构复杂性和计算难解性的问题，本文旨在在保证部分嵌套信息结构的前提下，实现JCCO问题的可计算性与最优性。

Method: 采用共同信息框架下的动态规划方法，分析开放环与闭环通信策略下部分嵌套信息结构的保持条件，并推导相应的Riccati方程以求解最优控制策略。

Result: 提出了确保部分嵌套性保持的系列条件，得到了开放环通信下的闭式Riccati解，并将方法推广到闭环通信情形，获得了比无穷维信念模型更易处理的动态规划形式。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效解决了具有部分嵌套信息结构的JCCO问题，为去中心化线性二次控制提供了新的求解路径，尤其在输出反馈和闭-loop通信场景下更具可操作性。

Abstract: In this paper, we formalize a joint communication-control strategy optimization (JCCO) problem in multi-agent linear systems with quadratic costs, under the common-information-based (CIB) framework from decentralized stochastic control. For computational tractability, we focus on such JCCO problems with partially nested (PN) information structures (ISs). In particular, with a baseline communication protocol that leads to a PN IS, we establish a series of conditions under which the partial nestedness is preserved under the (additional) communication strategies to be optimized, while violating them may cause nonlinearity of the optimal strategies in general, with open-loop communication strategies. We then develop a dynamic-programming-based approach to compute the optimal control strategies of JCCO with open-loop communication strategies, which yields a set of closed-form Riccati Equations. As a byproduct of independent interest, such an approach also offers a way to solve decentralized linear-quadratic control with PN ISs and output feedback, under the CIB framework. Finally, we extend such an approach to JCCOs with closed-loop communication strategies, yielding a more tractable dynamic program than an infinite-dimensional CIB-belief-based one.

</details>


<div id='quant-ph'></div>

# quant-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [113] [Embedding Stabilizer Codes and Leakage Correction in Multilevel Quantum Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12450)
*Ali Abu-Nada,Lian-Ao Wu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种在多能级量子系统中统一的量子纠错框架，通过等距嵌入保持纠错结构，并证明了泄漏可纠正的充要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决多能级量子硬件中超出计算子空间的泄漏错误问题，以提升量子纠错能力。

Method: 将任意[[n,k,d]]稳定子码等距嵌入到单个D维系统中，并推导出精确泄漏纠正的充要条件。

Result: 证明了当泄漏不区分逻辑态时，泄漏是可纠正的，从而建立了多能级系统中的统一量子纠错框架。

Conclusion: 任何[[n,k,d]]稳定子码都可以在保持其完整纠错结构的同时嵌入高维系统，且泄漏错误可在特定条件下被完全纠正。

Abstract: Leakage beyond the computational subspace is a major source of error in multilevel quantum hardware. We show that any \( [[n,k,d]] \) stabilizer code can be embedded isometrically into a single \(D\)-dimensional system while preserving its complete error-correcting structure. We further derive a necessary and sufficient condition for exact leakage correction, proving that leakage is correctable precisely when it does not distinguish between logical states. These results establish a unified framework for quantum error correction in multilevel quantum systems.

</details>


### [114] [Aperiodicity is sufficient for macroscopic thermalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13462)
*Amit Vikram*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种宏观可观测量在有限时间内热化的通用机制，基于量子动力学初态的非周期性和部分遍历性探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在将宏观热化纳入可操作的量子统计力学框架，提供有限时间内的热化预测。

Method: 通过分析初态系综的回返概率和希尔伯特空间中的动态探索特性，推导出宏观可观测量的平衡条件。

Result: 证明了在大多数初态和时间段内，所有集中的宏观可观测量都会达到平衡，并涵盖了无限时间热化的标准结果。

Conclusion: 该机制为有限时间和热力学极限下的量子热化提供了严格且可计算的描述，扩展了传统本征态热化的理论。

Abstract: We identify a general mechanism for the finite-time thermalization of macroscopic observables, such as coarse-grained charge densities, in terms of elementary forms of the quantum dynamics of initial states: (1) aperiodicity, which provides a computable measure of (2) a dynamical partially ergodic exploration of the Hilbert space. Specifically, this mechanism predicts the equilibration of all (concentrated) macroscopic observables, in almost all states in an initial ensemble and almost all times within finite and longer intervals, given only the observable-independent information that the return probability of the ensemble of initial states is small over a finite time range. As a special case, it also accesses standard results on equilibration over infinitely long times in terms of (stronger versions of) the effective dimension of initial state delocalization in the energy eigenbasis. Our results incorporate macroscopic thermalization into the domain of operational quantum statistical mechanics, recently developed to provide finitely computable criteria for microscopic thermalization.
