We discuss an overall characterization of this approach as establishing connections between (1) the decay of a (theoretically or experimentally) computable probe indicating memorylessness, (2) a fundamental invariant mechanism in terms of the alignment of observables or states in the Hilbert space, and (3) predicting different natural forms of (classical and) quantum thermalization, most of which rigorously recover conventional eigenstate-based descriptions of infinite-time thermalization as a special case but provide stronger accessible predictions over finite observation times in the thermodynamic limit.

</details>


### [115] [Spatially Dense, Continuous-Variable Quantum Computing with Solid State Spin Nonlinearities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12504)
*Hamza Raniwala,Ethan G Arnault,Dirk R. Englund,Matthew E. Trusheim*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种利用应变敏感的固态自旋作为非线性元件实现机械猫态量子比特间近邻连接的玻色量子信息处理架构，具有高逻辑量子比特密度和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机械模式通常缺乏必要的非线性来实现玻色通道间的相互作用，限制了其在多量子比特量子计算中的应用。

Method: 利用应变敏感的固态自旋作为非线性元件，结合纳米机械谐振器和自旋量子比特，构建可在片上实现近邻连接、控制和读出的玻色量子信息处理架构。

Result: 实现了机械猫态量子比特之间的近邻连接，展示了高空间密度的逻辑量子比特布局，并明确了实现高密度下容错所需的性能指标。

Conclusion: 该架构为基于纳米机械系统的可扩展量子信息处理提供了可行路径，结合玻色纠错和小型化器件，有望实现高效的量子计算。

Abstract: Nanomechanical structures have been investigated as a method of achieving long-lived quantum excitations at radio frequencies. Their high quality factors are especially intriguing as a medium for bosonic encoding of quantum information. However, to leading order, mechanical modes typically lack the nonlinearities necessary to achieve interaction between bosonic channels and thus are limited in their ability to scale to the many-qubit regime necessary for practical quantum computing. In this work, we propose and describe an approach for bosonic quantum information processing that uses strain-sensitive solid-state spins as nonlinear elements to produce the relevant nonclassical mechanical states. We outline the architecture required to achieve nearest-neighbor connectivity between mechanical cat-state qubits on-chip, as well as the control and readout architecture required for universal quantum computation. In addition, we show that this architecture can allow for a high spatial density of logical qubits by leveraging both the efficiency of bosonic error correction schemes and the small sizes of the constituent nanomechanical resonators and spin qubits. Finally, we identify the necessary performance metrics that will enable error-correction thresholds at high qubit densities, illuminating a path towards scalable quantum information processing.

</details>


### [116] [Quantum Tanner Codes at Moderate Blocklength](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12509)
*Feroz Ahmed Mian,Vaishnavi L. Addala,Arman Meraj,Adhiraj Chadha,Stefan Krastanov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了具有优良率和距离的量子Tanner码的显式构造，通过左右Cayley复形描述和“提升”视角两种互补方法实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在中等块长度范围内找到具有更优距离特性的量子纠错码，研究者探索了非阿贝尔群上的量子Tanner码构造。

Method: 采用左右Cayley复形（LRCC）描述以及基于有限群作用的CSS码“提升”方法，并利用GAP的小群库进行广泛搜索，结合随机化距离估计算法sQetch评估码距。

Result: 发现了多个新码实例，其距离上界超过20，例如[[480,8,(≤21,≤21)]]等，校验权重为9到20之间；使用Tesseract解码器估计出在不同噪声模型下的伪阈值。

Conclusion: 所提出的量子Tanner码在中等长度下表现出良好的距离和错误纠正性能，且开源工具QuantumExpanders.jl支持进一步研究与应用。

Abstract: We present explicit constructions of quantum Tanner (QT) codes with good rate and distance, obtained through two complementary approaches: the left-right Cayley complex (LRCC) description and the "lifting" perspective, in which a seed Calderbank-Shor-Steane (CSS) code is lifted by commuting left-right regular actions of a finite group $\mathcal{G}$. Through an extensive search over non-abelian groups from GAP's SmallGrp library, we investigate the moderate-blocklength regime ($n \in [500,1000]$) and identify several new code instances with distance upper bounds exceeding $20$. These include $[[480,8,(\leq 21,\leq 21)]]$, $[[504,4,(\leq 36,\leq 27)]]$, $[[672,4,(\leq 48,\leq 28)]]$, $[[720,6,(\leq 30,\leq 30)]]$, and $[[864,8,(\leq 39,\leq 31)]]$, with these bounds obtained using up to $350$ million trials of sQetch, a randomized distance estimator. The code instances presented have check weights ranging from $9$ to $20$. Using the Tesseract decoder, we estimate pseudo-thresholds of $3.6\%$-$4.6\%$ under phenomenological noise and $0.14\%$-$0.27\%$ under circuit-level noise, comparable to prior results at shorter code lengths. We also provide QuantumExpanders.jl, an open-source Julia library for constructing QT codes and explicit constructions of Ramanujan graphs.

</details>


### [117] [Flux control of measurement back-action and Leggett-Garg correlations in chiral quantum walks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12519)
*Paolo Luppi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究了手性连续时间量子行走中规范不变通量对单顶点序贯测量的干涉效应，发现通量可通过闭合路径干涉调控时间关联和测量反作用，并提出利用通量工程实现Lüders界饱和的通用机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索规范不变通量如何影响量子行走中的时间关联与测量反作用，特别是在序贯测量下的Leggett-Garg关联和局域动力学行为。

Method: 采用二分法可观测量区分返回目标顶点与占据其他位置的情况，结合返回振幅精确计算双时间统计量，分析通量对闭合行走干涉、谱测度及Krylov子空间结构的影响。

Result: 短时间扰动主导项与Peierls相位无关，高阶项通过闭合路径干涉体现通量敏感性；提出并实现了使局部动力学退化为等权重二维Krylov子空间以达到Lüders界3/2的机制；在双通量菱形图上精确实现该机制，且局域反作用依赖于两个独立通量的相对组合；对于含通量环路，展开显示偶数环在t^N阶、奇数环在t^{2N}阶出现主导通量对比。

Conclusion: 规范不变通量可作为调控局域测量反作用和时间量子关联的有效资源，提供了通过通量工程优化Leggett-Garg不等式违背程度和时序响应的新途径。

Abstract: Gauge-invariant fluxes control interference in chiral continuous-time quantum walks. We investigate how they affect sequential measurements at a single vertex, using a dichotomic observable that distinguishes return to that vertex from occupation of its complement. For a walker initially localized at the measured vertex, the complete two-time statistics, including measurement back-action and Leggett--Garg correlators, are determined exactly by the return amplitude, connecting temporal correlations to the local spectral measure and gauge-invariant closed-walk interference. At short times, the leading disturbance is independent of the Peierls phases, whereas flux sensitivity enters at higher orders through interference among closed walks. We further identify a graph-independent sufficient mechanism for saturating the Lüders bound: flux can reduce the rooted dynamics to a balanced two-dimensional Krylov subspace with equal spectral weights, yielding a constructive flux-engineering criterion for attaining the Lüders bound of $3/2$ at finite times. The mechanism is realized exactly on a two-flux diamond graph, where destructive interference renders additional rooted modes dark and the local back-action depends on relative combinations of the two independent fluxes. For flux-threaded cycles, an exact winding-number expansion reveals a parity-dependent onset: the leading flux contrast occurs generically at order $t^N$ for even cycles and $t^{2N}$ for odd cycles. Across the cycles examined, flux can either enhance the maximal Leggett--Garg violation or shift strong violations to earlier measurement times, with half flux driving the four-site cycle to the Lüders bound. These results establish gauge-invariant flux as a resource for engineering local measurement back-action and temporal quantum correlations.

</details>


### [118] [AutoQuREO: A Framework for Automated Quantum Resource Estimation and Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12936)
*Harshkumar Oza,Aritra Sarkar,Syed Naqi Abbas,Rahul Bhowmick,Aryan Prakash,Prateek P Kulkarni,Krishna Kumar Sabapathy*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了AutoQuREO，一个用于量子计算全栈资源估计与优化的自动化框架，通过抽象化、模块化组件、代理建模和多目标优化，支持在复杂设计空间中进行高效探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子资源估计方法依赖于繁重的编译过程或领域知识引导的符号标注，且多局限于容错假设，难以适应实际应用场景的需求。

Method: 提出AutoQuREO框架，包含四方面创新：用户可定义的量子计算栈抽象、可复用组件库、基于算法分析和神经符号学习的层间资源代理建模，以及集成多目标优化功能。

Result: 在多种共设计案例中验证了AutoQuREO的能力，包括近故障容许算法、小规模纠错码、门分解和变分量子电路训练，展示了其发现传统工具难以捕捉的资源权衡的优势。

Conclusion: AutoQuREO作为一个通用平台，能够推动量子技术从理论走向实用，提升系统级优化能力。

Abstract: As quantum computing progresses from proof-of-principle demonstrations toward practical utility, a significant impediment is the need to augment algorithmic feasibility with system-level optimization across heterogeneous hardware and software stacks. Quantum resource estimation (QRE) plays a central role in this transition, yet existing approaches remain largely compilation-heavy or domain-knowledge-guided symbolic annotations, and tightly coupled to long-term fault-tolerant assumptions, limiting their topical applicability.
  In this work, we introduce AutoQuREO, an Automated framework for full-stack Quantum Resource Estimation and Optimization. AutoQuREO is built around four core novelties: (i) a flexible, user-defined abstraction of the quantum computing stack; (ii) a modular library of reusable stack components enabling rapid full-stack prototyping; (iii) surrogate modeling of layer-wise resources via algorithmic profiling and neuro-symbolic learning; and (iv) integrated multi-objective optimization that embeds QRE directly into deployment pipelines. Together, these design choices enable AutoQuREO to serve as a digital twin for quantum computing stacks, supporting the tractable exploration of complex design spaces.
  We demonstrate the capabilities of AutoQuREO through representative co-design case studies, including early-fault-tolerant quantum algorithms, small error correction codes, gate decomposition and variational training of parametric quantum circuits. These examples illustrate how AutoQuREO enables systematic discovery of unexploited resource trade-offs that are computationally intractable or abstruse using existing QRE tools. AutoQuREO is positioned as a general-purpose platform for advancing quantum technology readiness.

</details>


### [119] [Coherent temporal filtering of multimode parametric down-conversion using a quantum pulse gate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12544)
*Abhinandan Bhattacharjee,Patrick Folge,Laura Serino,Sebastian Lengeling,Benjamin Brecht,Christine Silberhorn*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究利用量子脉冲门（QPG）对多模参量下转换（PDC）光源进行相干时间滤波，成功生成纯度超过0.90的纯单光子，显著优于传统光谱强度滤波方法，并展示了其在提取复杂时间模式中的灵活性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于大多数实际单光子源发射的是光谱多模态、纯度较低的光子，限制了量子网络中高可见度干涉的实现，因此需要一种能选择明确定义时间模式并提高光子纯度的有效滤波方法。

Method: 采用量子脉冲门（QPG）对具有强光谱关联的多模PDC光源进行相干时间滤波，并与传统光谱强度滤波进行对比，使用chronocyclic Q函数层析技术测量光子纯度。

Result: QPG滤波可稳定生成纯度高于0.90的触发单光子，且不受滤波形状影响；而传统强度滤波仅产生混合态光子。此外，QPG还能灵活提取包括皮秒级时间-bin叠加在内的结构化时间模式。

Conclusion: 量子脉冲门是一种实用且高效的相干滤波工具，适用于未来量子网络中高质量单光子的生成与操控。

Abstract: Spectrally pure and indistinguishable single photons are essential for quantum network platforms, where high-visibility interference underpins many quantum information protocols. However, most practical single-photon sources emit spectrally multimode states with reduced purity. Conventional spectral intensity filtering can partially improve purity but cannot select a well-defined temporal mode (TM). Here, we demonstrate coherent temporal filtering of a multimode parametric down-conversion (PDC) source using a quantum pulse gate (QPG) and benchmark its performance against conventional intensity filtering. The generated PDC photons exhibit strong spectral correlations, rendering extraction of pure heralded photons from the pair a challenge. We demonstrate that QPG filtering consistently generates heralded photons with purities above 0.90 regardless of the filter shape. Contrariwise, using spectral intensity filters yields mixed photons. Photon purities are probed with chronocyclic Q-function tomography. Furthermore, we demonstrate the versatility of QPG filtering by extracting structured TMs, including superposition of picosecond time bins. These results establish the QPG as a practical coherent filtering tool for quantum network applications.

</details>


### [120] [Quantum-Inspired Phase Bicoherence Spectroscopy: A Framework for Detecting Universal Textural Angular Order Across Multi-Modal Complex Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13342)
*Zheng Xing,Chan-Tong Lam,Xiaochen Yuan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出量子相位双相干（QPBC）光谱法，用于提取图像中与规范不变相关的角序信息，无需显式相位重建，在多种生物医学图像数据集中展现出优越的纹理分析能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图像分析丢弃了傅里叶相位中的方向性信息，且现有方法难以在不重建相位的情况下提取规范不变的角相关性，限制了对细胞结构中旋转鲁棒特征的刻画。

Method: 将图像的角度扇区嵌入九量子比特纠缠态，通过辅助量子比特探测三体双相干，获得16个可解释读出通道，实现对规范不变角序的捕捉。

Result: 在荧光、明场和组织病理学三个公开数据集上验证，QPBC能稳定分辨角相位序并显著区分不同生物表型；主轴对齐后最优探测频率收敛，负对照实验确认其开关效应，且性能优于传统傅里叶相位统计方法。

Conclusion: QPBC提供了一种普适的、经典无法实现的定量纹理观测量，建立了可解释的量子形态学，拓展了适用于多模态显微成像的量子启发分析工具箱。

Abstract: Classical image analysis routinely discards structurally meaningful orientation signatures encoded within Fourier phase, which are easily corrupted by local cellular rotation. Although quantum-inspired data processing offers new avenues for complex signal characterization, practical tools for directly extracting gauge-invariant angular correlations without explicit phase reconstruction remain scarce. Here we introduce Quantum Phase Bicoherence (QPBC) spectroscopy, a novel quantum-interferometric framework for capturing gauge-invariant angular order. The method embeds image angular sectors into a nine-qubit entangled state and probes three-body bicoherence via an ancilla, yielding 16 interpretable readout channels. We validate our framework on three independent public multi-modal imaging datasets covering fluorescence (BBBC021), bright-field (BBBC041) and histopathology (PathMNIST). QPBC consistently resolves angular-phase order and discriminates distinct biological phenotypes with high statistical significance. After principal-axis alignment, the optimal probing frequency universally converges, driven by Fourier directional sensitivity; negative-control experiments fully eliminate discriminative capacity, demonstrating frequency tuning acts as an on-off switch. Cross-dataset benchmarks confirm QPBC outperforms conventional Fourier-phase statistics, where inherent inversion symmetry serves as a built-in pipeline self-check. QPBC delivers a universal, classically unachievable quantitative texture observable, establishes interpretable quantum morphometry, and broadens the toolbox for quantum-inspired analysis applicable to diverse multi-modal microscopic measurements.

</details>


### [121] [Pulse Engineering of Quantum Many-Body Dynamics: Emergent Scar States, Entanglement, and Nonstabilizerness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12559)
*Prasant Mallik,Arkaprava Sil,Sudipto Singha Roy*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究利用脉冲工程技术构建了一个可在混沌海森堡链与ZX哈密顿量之间连续调节的有效哈密顿量，实现了对量子多体系统中非典型本征态和量子资源的精确控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非平衡量子物理中，如何系统设计并连续调控相互作用量子多体系统的性质仍是一个挑战，尤其是在单一微观模型中实现对量子混沌、非典型本征态和量子资源的协同调控。

Method: 采用脉冲工程技术构造有效哈密顿量，使其在含局域杂质的混沌XXX链与ZX哈密顿量之间连续插值，并分析插值过程中出现的可解析处理的非典型本征态及其纠缠与非稳定化特性。

Result: 发现了多种具有不同纠缠和非稳定化特性的非典型本征态家族：在XXX极限下出现精确的纠缠平台；随着脉冲增强，低能谱中出现新的近似纠缠平台层级；在ZX极限下发现一对长程纠缠的彩虹疤痕态。脉冲工程保持了原模型的混沌与非混沌区域差异，且在两类区域中生成几乎相同的量子资源。

Conclusion: 脉冲工程技术为构建具有结构化本征态和可调量子资源的多体哈密顿量提供了通用而灵活的框架，揭示了量子混沌与量子资源生成之间的部分解耦。

Abstract: Understanding and coherently controlling the properties of interacting quantum many-body systems is a central challenge in non-equilibrium quantum physics. While in the past decades, a wide range of many-body Hamiltonians have been introduced to study quantum chaos, atypical eigenstates, and quantum resources, systematically engineering and continuously tuning these properties within a single microscopic model remains largely unexplored. Here, we employ a pulse engineering scheme to construct an effective Hamiltonian that continuously interpolates between a chaotic Heisenberg (XXX) chain with a local impurity and the ZX Hamiltonian. Along this interpolation, we identify several families of analytically tractable atypical eigenstates embedded in the excited-state spectrum with distinct entanglement and nonstabilizerness properties. In the XXX limit, these states exhibit exact plateaus in both entanglement and stabilizer Rényi entropy and correspond to coherent superpositions of long-range valence-bond solid (VBS) states. As the pulse strength increases, the effective Hamiltonians exhibit a hierarchy of new set of approximate entanglement plateaus in the low-energy spectrum. Interestingly, in the fully pulse-engineered ZX limit, we uncover a distinct pair of long-range entangled stabilizer eigenstates, corresponding to rainbow scar states. We further show that pulse engineering preserves the distinct chaotic and non-chaotic regimes of the original model, while that is largely absent in the dynamical generation of entanglement and nonstabilizerness. The pulse-engineered models generate nearly identical quantum resources in both regimes, revealing a partial decoupling between quantum chaos and quantum-resource generation. Our results establish pulse engineering as a versatile framework for generating many-body Hamiltonians with structured eigenstates and tunable quantum resources.

</details>


### [122] [Time-ordered free energy in correlated quantum systems: An agentic approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12942)
*Ruo Cheng Huang,Isha Singh Le Xue,Yuxuan Qu,Paul M. Riechers,Varun Narasimhachar,Mile Gu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种在因果约束下从非马尔可夫量子态序列中提取最大功的最优策略，并引入了时序自由能（TOFE）作为衡量时序关联量子系统中可用自由能的基本度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在无法观测底层隐马尔可夫机器的情况下，受限于在线操作和因果约束的智能体如何从非马尔可夫量子态序列中提取能量。

Method: 结合动态规划和计算力学技术，设计并识别出最优的智能体策略，其时间复杂度与序列长度呈线性关系。

Result: 提出了时序自由能（TOFE）这一新度量，能够量化在因果约束下可提取的最大功。

Conclusion: TOFE为评估时序关联量子系统中的可用自由能提供了基础框架，且该方法在计算上是高效的。

Abstract: How much work can an agent extract from a temporal sequence of quantum states when it can only operate online under causal constraints---deciding which energy extraction method to use with knowledge of what it has observed before? Here, we study this problem in the context of quantum state sequences that are potentially non-Markovian---generated by some underlying hidden Markov machine that the agent cannot observe. Using techniques from dynamic programming and computational mechanics, we present a method to identify the provably optimal agent strategy, with time complexity that scales linearly with sequence length. This motivates us to introduce the maximum work such agents can extract---time-ordered free energy(TOFE)---as a fundamental measure of free energy available in a temporally correlated quantum system subject to causal considerations.

</details>


### [123] [Two-atom Dicke model with atom-atom interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12625)
*Lin Jiao,Han Pu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究了两个相互作用原子与单模腔耦合的Dicke模型中相互作用对超辐射临界性的影响，发现相互作用可改变相变边界、抑制原子-光子耦合阈值并改变相变的普适类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨发射体间的相互作用在超辐射临界性中的作用，目前这一领域尚未被充分研究。

Method: 构建了一个最小模型——两个相互作用原子耦合到单模腔的Dicke模型，并进行解析分析。

Result: 发现相互作用能改变相边界，甚至完全抑制超辐射的原子-光子耦合阈值，并改变相变的普适类；同时研究了耗散通道（如光子损耗和自旋弛豫）下的耗散相变和量子动力学。

Conclusion: 原子间相互作用在调控原子-光子耦合中起关键作用，且可通过工程化设计来控制超辐射相变行为。

Abstract: Interactions among emitters provide a powerful means of controlling collective light--matter phenomena, yet their role in superradiant criticality has not been thoroughly investigated. Here we construct a minimal model that can yield analytical insights --- a Dicke model with two interacting atoms coupled to a single mode cavity --- to study such interaction effects. We show that interaction changes the phase boundary, and may even completely suppress the atom-photon coupling threshold for superradiance and change the universality class of the phase transition. We further study the dissipative phase transition and quantum dynamics in the presence of dissipation channels such as photon loss and spin relaxation. Our results demonstrate important roles played by the atom-atom interaction and how it can be engineered to control atom-photon coupling.

</details>


### [124] [Free-Space Quantum Networks and Optimized Fiber-Reinforcement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12636)
*Alasdair I. Fletcher,Ignazio Pedone,Stefano Pirandola*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了大规模自由空间量子网络的端到端性能，提出通过优化布置少量光纤骨干节点来显著提升网络容量和降低边消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自由空间量子通信受衍射、大气衰减和湍流影响，点对点容量受限，需提升网络整体性能。

Method: 建模为Waxman随机图，分析单路径与多路径路由下的网络容量、边消耗及连通性相变；引入基于Voronoi分割和Delaunay三角化的光纤骨干节点优化布局。

Result: 推导出星型路由器网络的端到端容量分布；证明最优骨干配置对应于容量最大化Voronoi分割，并可通过Lloyd算法逼近；数值结果表明少量骨干节点可显著提升性能。

Conclusion: 结合少量优化布置的光纤骨干节点能有效增强自由空间量子网络的容量与效率，适用于固定和移动用户场景。

Abstract: Free-space quantum communication provides a flexible complement to fiber-based quantum networks, but its point-to-point capacity is fundamentally limited by diffraction, atmospheric extinction and beam wandering induced by turbulence. In this work, we study the end-to-end performance of large-scale free-space quantum networks connecting randomly distributed fixed or mobile users, modelled as Waxman random graphs. We derive the mean network capacity, edge consumption and connectivity phase transitions for both single-path and multi-path (flooding) routing. We also study router-centered star networks, deriving the full distribution of end-to-end capacities as a function of the router's coverage radius. We then consider how performance may be improved by reinforcing free-space networks with a small number of optimally placed fiber-based backbone nodes. We prove that any optimal backbone configuration must correspond to a capacity-maximizing Voronoi tessellation of the network region, and show that this can be efficiently approximated by a centroidal Voronoi tessellation via Lloyd's algorithm, with backbone nodes connected according to a Delaunay triangulation. Numerical results show that even a modest number of backbone nodes substantially improves end-to-end capacity and reduces edge consumption for both mobile and fixed users.

</details>


### [125] [Topological State-Aware Simulation Framework for Inter-Satellite Twin-Field QKD Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12659)
*Sergio Vázquez-Pozo,Juan Manuel Murillo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于拓扑数据分析（TDA）的参考型后选择机制，用于解决星间链路中TF-QKD在动态轨道环境下因非独立同分布噪声导致的密钥率崩溃问题，通过蜂群信标数据构建公开接受事件，并开发了带安全账本的模拟框架进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自适应后选择方法在满足严格独立性条件时存在缺陷或引入严重二阶惩罚，导致有限密钥分析下密钥率崩溃，难以适用于高动态、非IID的卫星轨道环境。

Method: 将卫星星座建模为细胞层（Cellular Sheaf），利用拓扑数据分析（TDA）从经典信标遥测数据中提取公共接受事件Ω，设计参考-only的拓扑后选择预言机，并构建包含随机噪声注入和显式GEAT安全账本的模块化仿真框架进行验证。

Result: 在2000-5000公里ISL距离下仿真显示，启用TDA后中位条件候选速率分别为2.14e-6和5.87e-7比特每脉冲，但在3000公里以上均为零；TDA在所有4788个窗口中均激活，但未扩展正候选范围。输出速率仅为条件数值候选，未完成全协议级可组合安全证明。

Conclusion: 所提拓扑后选择机制能有效生成符合独立性要求的公共事件，避免传统方法的安全隐患，尽管当前未提升有效通信距离，但为未来实现高稳健性星载量子密钥分发提供了可验证的架构基础。

Abstract: Inter-satellite links (ISLs) are the mandatory backbone for global quantum networks. While Twin-Field Quantum Key Distribution (TF-QKD) successfully surpasses linear rate-loss bounds, its extreme phase sensitivity makes it highly vulnerable to dynamic, non-IID (Independent and Identically Distributed) orbital environments. In composable finite-key analyses governed by the Generalized Entropy Accumulation Theorem (GEAT), traditional adaptive post-selection heuristics either violate strict independence conditions or incur massive second-order penalties that collapse the secret key rate. To overcome this, we introduce a reference-only topological post-selection oracle. By modeling the constellation as a Cellular Sheaf and applying Topological Data Analysis (TDA), our protocol derives a public acceptance event ($Ω$) exclusively from classical beacon telemetry. To rigorously validate this mechanism, we develop a modular simulation framework equipped with stochastic noise injection and an explicit GEAT security ledger. Simulations across 2,000-5,000 km ISL separations compare the same Hodge-Koopman gate with TDA disabled and enabled. At 2,000 km, the median conditional candidate rates are $2.14 \times 10^{-6}$ and $5.87 \times 10^{-7}$ bit per emitted pulse, respectively; both configurations return zero at 3,000-5,000 km. TDA is active in all 4,788 evaluated windows, but does not extend the positive-candidate range in this scenario. These exported rates are conditional numerical candidates: the full protocol-level composable-security proof remains incomplete and the certified composable rate is therefore zero throughout.

</details>


### [126] [SPLIT-Q: A Scalable Sequential Quantum Computing Framework for Coherent Controlled Islanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12711)
*Yuqi Jiang,Zhiding Liang,Qiang Guan,Yan Li,Ganesh Kumar Venayagamoorthy*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于量子近似优化算法（QAOA）的分步分布式框架，用于在有限量子资源下实现电力系统的受控孤岛划分，具有可扩展性和较低的量子资源需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着分布式能源资源的增加，电力系统在扰动下的不确定性和不平衡问题加剧，传统孤岛划分方法计算复杂度高，而单体量子优化方法对量子比特需求大，难以扩展。

Method: 提出一种受量子比特限制的顺序分布式QAOA框架，将全局优化问题分解为多个区域性的二次无约束二值优化（QUBO）子问题，依次求解，并通过边界条件保证整体一致性；电路宽度不随网络规模增长，在固定量子比特预算内运行。

Result: 在11个IEEE标准系统（9至300节点）上验证，使用IBM量子资源，该方法在噪声环境下仍能恢复出与Gurobi最优解一致的可行孤岛方案；相比单体QAOA显著降低了量子资源消耗和电路复杂度。

Conclusion: 该框架为大规模电力系统中的量子优化提供了可行且可扩展的路径，能够在当前量子硬件条件下处理大型受控孤岛问题。

Abstract: Growing integration of distributed energy resources increases power-system variability and uncertainty. During disturbances, these effects can intensify generation-load imbalances and cascading failures. Controlled islanding limits their propagation by partitioning a compromised grid into connected, electrically sustainable islands. However, classical methods face rapidly growing computational costs as network size and island count increase. Quantum optimization offers an alternative for exploring this combinatorial partition space. Yet monolithic quantum formulations encode all assignment decisions in one circuit, causing qubit demand and circuit complexity to scale with network size. In this study, a qubit-bounded sequential distributed quantum approximate optimization algorithm (QAOA) framework is proposed to tackle coherent controlled islanding under limited quantum resources. It formulates the optimization as boundary-conditioned regional quadratic unconstrained binary optimization (QUBO) subproblems that are solved sequentially within a fixed qubit budget. Thus, circuit width remains independent of network size, with aggregate quantum workload scaling linearly on bounded-degree networks. Evaluation covers eleven IEEE systems from 9 to 300 buses using IBM quantum computing resources, with Gurobi and monolithic QAOA as references. Across all systems, the framework recovers feasible Gurobi-optimal partitions under noise, confirming the resilience of its solution quality. The results further show that the proposed method substantially reduces quantum-resource demand and circuit complexity relative to monolithic QAOA, allowing large islanding problems to be addressed within current hardware limits. The proposed framework provides a feasible and scalable pathway for quantum optimization in large-scale power systems.

</details>


### [127] [Parity Floors in Quantum Denoisers: A Closed-Form Benchmark for Fixed-Map Denoising Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12712)
*Jaeuk Kim*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的torus-diffusion基准CoupledPhaseTexture，用于分析固定量子特征映射在扩散去噪器中的结构性限制，发现深度1的RY+CNOT+Pauli-Z族存在无包含的奇偶性下限，导致所有可达特征为编码角度的偶函数，而贝叶斯去噪目标的正弦分量为奇函数，从而产生不可约的奇部分和部门内残差，该下限在不同噪声尺度下均成立，并且与特征类的静态可表示性无关；实验表明，高阶Z读出能改善偶数部门但纠缠不能降低下限，重复上传也不能可靠地消除它，经典控制实验证明缺陷源于奇偶性而非量子性；结果强调需要非经典数据访问或没有高效经典替代的特征类，但未证明这些足以实现量子优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准图像基准无法揭示固定量子特征映射在扩散去噪器中受何种结构约束限制，因此需要一个新的基准来分离奇偶性、部门内逼近能力和样本复杂度等不同因素的影响。

Method: 引入CoupledPhaseTexture这一torus-diffusion基准，结合解析热核加噪方法，分析深度1的RY+CNOT+Pauli-Z量子电路族的特征映射性质；通过理论证明奇偶性下限的存在，并在不同噪声尺度下推导其不变性；使用两类先验进行实验验证，比较不同读出方式（如高阶Z读出、奇读出）、纠缠和重上传策略的效果，并与仅含余弦的经典基组对比。

Result: 证明了深度1 RY+CNOT+Pauli-Z族存在一个不依赖于特征类包含、线性或闭合性的奇偶性下限：所有可达特征为偶函数，而贝叶斯去噪目标的正弦部分为奇函数，导致不可约的奇部分风险；实验显示该奇偶代理主导了超额风险；高阶Z读出可改善偶数部门表现，但纠缠无法降低下限，重上传也无法可靠消除；经典余弦基组同样受限，加入正弦项后即可匹配参考性能；测试的所有量子构造（包括奇读出和噪声耦合编码器）均未能匹配携带正弦的经典基组。

Conclusion: 奇偶性错配是当前固定量子特征映射的根本限制，该限制源于噪声条件下去噪目标的动态奇偶特性变化，而非静态表示能力；尽管提升量子电路结构（如纠缠、重上传）有一定作用，但不足以克服此下限；要实现潜在量子优势，需探索非经典数据访问方式或不具备高效经典模拟的特征类，但本文未证实这些条件足以达成量子优势。

Abstract: Fixed quantum feature maps are increasingly inserted into diffusion denoisers, but standard image benchmarks do not reveal which structural constraint limits them. We introduce CoupledPhaseTexture, a torus-diffusion benchmark with analytic heat-kernel noising that separates parity, within-sector approximation, and sample-complexity limitations. For the depth-1 RY+CNOT+Pauli-Z family we prove a containment-free parity floor: all reachable features are even functions of the encoded angles while the sine components of the Bayes denoiser are odd, so the excess risk splits exactly into an inaccessible odd part and a within-sector residual. The first term is an irreducible, noise-scale-resolved lower bound holding for every even feature class, with no containment, linearity, or closedness assumption on the feature class. The obstruction is a property of the noise-conditioned denoising target rather than static representability: the floor is re-derived at each noise scale because the target's parity content changes with noise. The measured excess is dominated by the parity proxy on two distinct priors. Higher-order Z readouts improve the even sector, but entanglement does not lower the floor and re-uploading does not reliably close it. Classical controls confirm the deficit is parity rather than quantumness: a cosine-only bank is floored similarly, while adding the sine sector matches the reference. Among tested constructions, odd readouts and a noise-coupled encoder do not match the sine-carrying classical bank. These results motivate nonclassical data access or feature classes without efficient classical surrogates; they do not establish either as sufficient for quantum advantage.

</details>


### [128] [Levitation of a YIG sphere using a magnetic Paul trap - towards strongly coupled quantum magno-mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12752)
*A. O. Yakymenko,S. Das,J. Twamley*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本研究利用磁性Paul阱在室温下实现了对钇铁石榴石（YIG）小球的稳定悬浮，并通过经典稳定性分析揭示了其平动与转动模式耦合对稳定区域的影响，实验获得较高的Q值和可观测的共振频率，数值模拟显示质心运动与磁振子模式间可实现强耦合（协作性大于1），尽管存在较强机械阻尼，仍有望应用于量子信息处理、量子互连与量子存储。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索宏观物体的无接触悬浮及其在量子技术中的应用潜力，特别是在室温下实现强耦合系统以推动量子传感与信息处理的发展。

Method: 采用磁性Paul阱对YIG微球进行捕获，结合经典稳定性理论分析系统的自由度稳定性，并通过实验测量悬浮性能（如Q因子和共振频率），辅以数值模拟评估质心运动与磁振子模式间的耦合强度。

Result: 成功在室温下实现YIG球体的稳定磁悬浮，测得Q因子约为25，共振频率分别为15.8 Hz和17.2 Hz；发现转动与平动模式耦合会改变稳定性区域；数值结果显示协作性可超过1，表明具备实现强耦合的潜力。

Conclusion: 磁性Paul阱可用于室温下软磁体的稳定悬浮，并能实现机械运动与磁振子间的增强耦合，为未来开发基于磁振子的量子器件提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Magnetic levitation offer passive levitation of massive objects for use in advanced inertia sensors, for the generation of non-classical macroscopic motional states, and towards the table-top testing of low energy gravity with quantum mechanics. Magnons, a quanta of spin wave, couple to many physical quantities and strongly to electromagnetic fields, even at room temperature. In this work we demonstrate the stable trapping of a small YIG sphere using a magnetic Paul trap. We present a classical stability analysis of a magnetic Paul trap and show the stability diagram for all mechanical degrees of freedom. We show that coupling between librational and translational modes changes the stability region. We experimentally levitate the soft magnet yttrium iron garnet at room temperature obtaining Q-factors of $\sim25$ and secular frequencies $15.8$ Hz and $17.2$ Hz. We provide numerical estimates of the achievable enhanced coupling between the center-of-mass motion and excited magnon modes, with a cooperativity above unity despite strong mechanical damping, indicating potential applications in quantum information processing, quantum interconnects and quantum memories.

</details>


### [129] [A Minimum-Cardinality Genuinely Unextendible Product Basis in Three Qutrits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12785)
*Fei Shi,Ge Bai,Xiande Zhang,Qi Zhao,Lvzhou Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文解决了是否存在真正不可扩展乘积基（GUPB）的开放问题，通过构造一个三量子三元系统中显式的十四维GUPB，并证明其为最小基数。该工作还展示了其在量子非局域性和纠缠态中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决GUPB是否存在这一长期开放问题，并确定最小可能的基数。

Method: 构造了一个具体的三量子三元系统的十四维GUPB，并结合低基数不存在性的证明得出最小性结论，进一步通过填充过程推广到其他系统。

Result: 证明了三量子三元系统中存在基数为十四的GUPB，且这是最小可能的基数；同时发现其正交补空间的归一化投影算子在每种二分法下都具有部分转置正性和束缚纠缠特性。

Conclusion: 十四是三量子三元系统中GUPB的最小基数，GUPB的存在性及其量子非局域性得到了证实。

Abstract: It has remained an open question whether a genuinely unextendible product basis (GUPB) exists. We resolve this problem by constructing an explicit three-qutrit GUPB of cardinality fourteen in the smallest tripartite Hilbert space in which a GUPB can exist. Together with the nonexistence of three-qutrit GUPBs of cardinality less than fourteen, our construction proves that fourteen is the minimum cardinality. A padding procedure further extends the construction to all tripartite systems whose local dimensions are at least three. As applications, the normalized projector onto the thirteen-dimensional orthogonal complement of the three-qutrit GUPB is positive under partial transposition and bound entangled across every bipartition, while the GUPB exhibits strong quantum nonlocality without entanglement.

</details>


### [130] [A Finite-Window Recovery Hierarchy for Local Quantum Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12803)
*Zheng An,Dongyang Cao,Jiangyu Cui*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为有限窗口可恢复性的操作性信道基准，用于区分量子信息在局部消失后是转移到邻近自由度还是变得无法通过浅层局域控制访问，并通过无序驱动伊辛模型和载波删除任务验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当存储在局部量子比特中的量子信息消失时，它可能并未丢失而是转移至附近自由度或难以被浅层局域控制获取；为了区分这两种情况并评估局域控制对量子记忆的恢复能力，需要一种新的操作性基准。

Method: 引入有限窗口可恢复性作为信道性能指标，结合目标位点恢复、有限窗口内有界深度解码器恢复与无约束最优恢复进行比较；采用局域变分恢复技术，利用局域态制备、窗口局域控制和目标量子比特泡利测量来认证超出目标位点的记忆恢复能力，并量化浅层控制可实现的部分。

Result: 在无序驱动伊辛弗洛凯链中，五位点窗口上的六层解码器实现了Q^opt_0 < Q^shallow_2 < Q^opt_2，在大多数无序实现中表现出正认证增益和较高的浅层可达性比例；信号不同于目标位点持续性和相干信息增量；使用独立张量网络后端验证了在更长开放链中嵌入任务时半径2增益仍存在；在载波删除任务中，八层解码器从半径3的周围区域恢复输入，中位平均保真度达0.758，超过单量子比特经典基准2/3，并优于一至三位点子窗口的反事实最优结果。

Conclusion: 有限窗口恢复作为一种局域控制基准，能够有效诊断局域量子信息是否保留在系统中以及是否有界深度控制能否将其重新聚焦，为离散时间系统中的局域量子记忆提供了实验上可行的探测手段。

Abstract: When quantum information initially stored in a local qubit disappears, it need not be lost: it may have moved into nearby degrees of freedom or become inaccessible to shallow local control. We introduce finite-window recoverability as an operational channel benchmark that separates these possibilities. It compares optimal recovery from the target site, recovery by a bounded-depth decoder on a finite window, and the unrestricted optimum for that window. Its operational component, local variational recovery, uses local state preparation, window-local control, and target-qubit Pauli readout to certify recoverable memory beyond the target and quantify how much of the same-window advantage is accessible to shallow control. In a disordered kicked-Ising Floquet chain, a depth-6 decoder on a five-site window realizes $Q^{\mathrm{opt}}_0<Q^{\mathrm{shallow}}_2<Q^{\mathrm{opt}}_2$ across the crossover regime, with positive certified gain for most disorder realizations and substantial shallow-accessibility fractions. The signal differs from target-site persistence and reconstructed coherent-information increments. Positive radius-2 gain also persists when the task is embedded in longer open chains using an independent tensor-network backend. Guided by this hierarchy, we test a carrier-deletion task in which the original target register is reset after the dynamics. A depth-8 decoder repairs the input from a radius-3 surrounding halo with held-out median $F_{\mathrm{avg}}=0.758$, above the single-qubit classical benchmark $2/3$, and outperforms optimal one-, two-, and three-site halo-subwindow counterfactuals. These results establish finite-window recovery as a local-control benchmark for off-site quantum memory, diagnosing both where local quantum information remains and whether bounded-depth control can refocus it.

</details>


### [131] [Sector-resolved non-Bloch topology and nonlocal entanglement dynamics in a bond-dissipative Kitaev chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12809)
*Shaina Gandhi,Koustav Roy,Bilal Tanatar,Saurabh Basu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在耗散拓扑超导体中，传统的非厄米体边对应失效，局域观测量无法反映拓扑性质；通过三量化形式下的键耗散二聚化Kitaev链模型，发现马约拉纳快度矩阵可分解为两个独立的非厄米部分，各自拥有广义布里渊区和非布洛赫缠绕数，揭示了分块分辨的非布洛赫体边对应。局域密度是跨扇区协方差，掩盖了单个扇区的拓扑特征；而纠缠谱的有限时间零事件可在周期边界下逐扇区恢复隐藏的边缘内容，作为动力学不变量揭示开边界快速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究耗散非厄米拓扑系统中局域观测量是否总能反映拓扑性质，探索传统非布洛赫体边对应在耗散拓扑超导体中的适用性。

Method: 采用三量化形式下的键耗散二聚化Kitaev链模型，分析马约拉纳快度矩阵的结构，识别其两个非厄米扇区各自的广义布里渊区与非布洛赫缠绕数，并研究局域密度和纠缠谱的动力学行为。

Result: 发现马约拉纳快度矩阵可分解为两个独立非厄米扇区，各自具有非布洛赫体边对应；局域密度因是跨扇区协方差而无法反映单一扇区的拓扑，导致传统诊断方法失效；纠缠谱的有限时间零事件可在周期边界演化下逐扇区恢复边缘模式，作为新的动力学拓扑不变量。

Conclusion: 在耗散拓扑超导体中，传统基于局域观测量的拓扑诊断失效，提出了扇区分辨的非布洛赫体边对应机制，并发现纠缠谱零事件可作为探测隐藏拓扑的动态不变量，无需实际打开边界。

Abstract: A core characteristic of dissipative non-Hermitian topology is that the relaxation dynamics tracks the non-Bloch bulk-boundary correspondence, rendering an algebraic decay in the gapless regime and an exponential falloff in the gapped phase, so that local observables directly diagnose the topology. We show that this correspondence breaks down in a dissipative topological superconductor, where the local observables turn blind to the very topology they are expected to decipher. Via a bond-dissipative dimerized Kitaev chain in a third-quantized rapidity-matrix formulation, we find that at zero chemical potential the Majorana rapidity matrix decomposes into two independent non-Hermitian sectors, each with its own generalized Brillouin zone and non-Bloch winding number, thereby revealing a sector-resolved non-Bloch bulk-boundary correspondence. The local density is a cross-sector covariance and relaxes at the sum of the two sector rates, so it remains sector-blind even when one sector is gapless and topological. For balanced gain and loss, the finite-time zero events of the entanglement spectrum under purely periodic-boundary Lindblad evolution recover this hidden edge content sector by sector, serving as a dynamical invariant that returns the open-boundary edge rapidities without physically opening the chain.

</details>


### [132] [Improved Measurement Cost Scaling in the Nonorthogonal Quantum Eigensolver](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12830)
*Mingyu Kang,K. Birgitta Whaley*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子子空间对角化方法在有限采样下的误差敏感性问题，提出通过重叠矩阵阈值化来控制本征值误差，并证明了测量成本的线性标度上界，显著优于此前已知的立方标度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子子空间对角化方法在近期量子计算机上具有应用前景，但其精度受限于重叠矩阵病态导致的本征值误差放大问题，亟需分析并降低有限采样误差对结果的影响。

Method: 作者分析了非正交量子本征求解器（NOQE）中有限采样误差的影响，引入重叠矩阵阈值化策略，并推导出一个有限采样扰动界，表明保留的重叠矩阵条件数决定了本征值敏感性。同时提出了可扩展的阈值方案。

Result: 理论证明在阈值化后，达到目标精度所需的每矩阵元测量次数上界为O(M)，优于此前的O(M^3)；氢链和氢环的数值实验表明实际测量成本增长可能比该线性界更慢。

Conclusion: 通过合理的重叠矩阵阈值化，NOQE等量子子空间方法可在近期设备上以较低测量成本实现可靠能量估计，为实际应用提供了理论支持和优化方向。

Abstract: Quantum subspace diagonalization methods are promising algorithms for quantum chemistry on near-term quantum computers. These methods can estimate low-lying energies of molecular systems using shallow quantum circuits, at the cost of many circuit repetitions to estimate the projected matrix elements. Errors in these matrix elements can be converted into much larger eigenvalue errors by an ill-conditioned overlap matrix. We study this bottleneck for the nonorthogonal quantum eigensolver (NOQE), which constructs a compact multireference subspace from dressed unrestricted Hartree-Fock states. We prove a finite-shot perturbation bound showing that, after overlap thresholding, the eigenvalue sensitivity is controlled by the condition number of the retained overlap matrix rather than by a worst-case dimension factor. With a scalable thresholding scheme, the upper bound on the per-matrix-element shot count required to reach a target accuracy scales as $\mathcal{O}(M)$, improving on the previously known $\mathcal{O}(M^3)$ bound, where $M$ is the number of reference states. Numerical experiments on hydrogen chains and rings suggest that, in practice, the measurement cost of structured NOQE instances can grow even more slowly than this linear bound.

</details>


### [133] [Shots-to-Approximate-Solution Scaling in Neutral-Atom Quantum Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12858)
*Junwoo Jung,Jaewook Ahn*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的度量方法STS(r)，用于评估中性原子量子优化协议在低能解结构上的集中性，并通过实验验证了量子退火在特定条件下优于随机基线，展示了量子优化的实际优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究中性原子量子优化协议是否真正趋向于低能解结构仍是一个开放问题，因此需要一种有效的方法来量化其性能表现。

Method: 引入了STS(r)度量指标，结合退火后处理输出的简并加权壳层分布模型，利用单个有效参数β表征向近最优独立集的集中性；同时构建激发匹配的随机基线以提取真实的量子集中效应。

Result: 在最多125个位点的里德堡原子阵列实验中，量子退火始终超过随机基线，显示出超越单纯激发密度的低能解结构集中增强；发现两种目标依赖的机制：对于接近精确的目标（r≈1），样本复杂度随系统规模指数增长，量子退火可将其降低相同指数水平；而对于宽松目标，经典后处理启发式方法即可在常数次尝试内达到目标。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一种可操作的量子优化性能量化方法，并明确了量子方法在何种条件下能带来实际优势，特别是在高精度目标下展现出显著的量子增强。

Abstract: Whether neutral-atom quantum optimization protocols exhibit genuine concentration toward low-energy solution structure remains an open question. Here, we introduce a shots-to-approximate-solution metric, STS(r), where r denotes the approximation ratio, and evaluate it using postprocessed outputs modeled by a degeneracy-weighted shell distribution governed by a single effective parameter, $β$, that quantifies concentration toward near-optimal independent sets. To extract the genuine concentration effect in the quantum data, we apply identical postprocessing to both experimental bitstrings and randomly generated bitstrings with matched excitation density, thereby constructing an excitation-matched random baseline. Experiments on programmable Rydberg-atom arrays with system sizes up to 125 sites show that quantum annealing consistently exceeds the random baseline, demonstrating enhanced concentration toward low-energy solution structure beyond what can be attributed solely to excitation density. The results further reveal two distinct target-dependent regimes. For near-exact targets with $r \approx 1$, the required shot count grows exponentially with system size and is reduced at the same exponential level by quantum annealing within the shell-model description. By contrast, for relaxed targets, the shot cost becomes effectively constant, and the corresponding quantum enhancement diminishes, with the classical postprocessing heuristic alone reaching the target in order-unity attempts. Together, these results establish an operational method for quantifying quantum optimization performance and clarify the regimes under which quantum approaches can yield practical benefits.

</details>


### [134] [Hybrid HPC-Quantum Simulations: DFT-Quantum Embedding for Molecular Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12884)
*Namrata Manglani,Samrit Maity,Shashank Sharma,Tejjan Arora,Soham Phulare,Shreyas Kadam,Sanjay Wandhekar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合经典DFT与量子嵌入的混合框架（QDFT），在无噪声模拟中验证其精度与可扩展性，为未来在真实量子硬件上的HPC-量子混合计算提供路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密度泛函理论（DFT）在处理强关联体系和带隙预测时存在近似限制，而当前量子计算受限于硬件资源与噪声，需发展结合二者优势的混合方法。

Method: 将大系统划分为活性空间与环境部分，活性区用变分量子本征求解器（VQE）处理，其余部分由DFT描述，并实现自洽密度更新与模块化耦合；采用无噪声量子模拟进行系统性评估，并建立QPU运行时间估算方法。

Result: 该框架能在保持经典HPC可扩展性的同时，提升部分电子结构性质的计算精度；性能分析揭示了CPU基量子模拟的瓶颈，并估算了实际QPU的执行需求。

Conclusion: QDFT框架展示了在大规模系统中融合量子嵌入的潜力，未来结合噪声抑制与实际量子硬件将推动实用化的HPC-量子混合模拟发展。

Abstract: Scientific simulations demand methods combining scalability with predictive accuracy. Density Functional Theory (DFT) on High-Performance Computing (HPC) enables large-scale electronic-structure simulations but is limited by approximations affecting strongly correlated systems and band-gap predictions. Quantum computing offers a pathway to address this, though current Noisy Intermediate-Scale Quantum (NISQ) hardware remains constrained by qubit resources, noise, and execution cost.
  This work presents a hybrid DFT-Quantum Embedding (QDFT) framework integrating classical HPC-based DFT with a quantum electronic-structure solver. Large systems are partitioned to isolate a chemically relevant active space, treated via the Variational Quantum Eigensolver (VQE), while the remaining degrees of freedom are described by DFT. The framework incorporates active-space selection, embedded Hamiltonian construction, symmetry preservation, operator mapping, self-consistent density updating, and modular classical-quantum coupling.
  We focus on noiseless quantum simulation to systematically evaluate accuracy, convergence, active-space dependence, computational cost, and HPC scalability without hardware noise. Detailed profiling identifies computational bottlenecks and highlights limitations of CPU-based quantum simulation. A QPU runtime-estimation methodology is additionally developed to assess execution requirements on actual quantum hardware.
  Results demonstrate quantum embedding's potential to improve selected electronic-structure properties while retaining classical HPC's scalability. Noisy quantum simulation and QPU execution remain key future directions, providing a pathway toward practical, scalable HPC-quantum hybrid simulations as hardware matures.

</details>


### [135] [Encoding Circuit Satisfiability in Rydberg Atom Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12938)
*Haotian Ji,Zhangjie Qin,Zheng An,Bowen Yan,Daoheng Niu,Kunzhe Dai,Jingkai Fang,Dongyang Cao,Jiangyu Cui*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为CAMERA的原子高效编码方法，将电路可满足性问题（Circuit-SAT）直接映射到里德堡原子阵列的MWIS问题中，显著降低了原子开销，并通过编译和模拟验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的Circuit-SAT到MWIS的转换依赖于合取范式（CNF），导致原子资源开销大，限制了里德堡原子阵列在组合优化问题中的应用效率。

Method: 提出CAMERA方法，将每个逻辑门表示为紧凑的加权小工具，并借鉴超大规模集成电路（VLSI）设计思想，开发放置与布线编译器，在里德堡阵列的王图子图结构上构建MWIS编码。

Result: 在随机多门基准测试中，相比CNF路线平均减少22.4倍的原子成本；成功编译全加器和乘法器并通过真值表验证；端到端演示了一个30原子实例的求解过程，包括编译、张量网络模拟和读出满足解。

Conclusion: CAMERA为在里德堡原子阵列上高效求解Circuit-SAT及更广泛的组合优化问题提供了切实可行的路径，并展示了从理论编码到硬件模拟的完整工作流程。

Abstract: Rydberg atom arrays natively encode the maximum-weight independent set (MWIS) problem through the blockade mechanism, so the Boolean circuit satisfiability problem (Circuit-SAT) can be brought onto the platform once it is reduced to MWIS. The conventional encoding of Circuit-SAT in the Rydberg atom array proceeds through conjunctive normal form (CNF) and incurs a substantial atom overhead. We introduce CAMERA (Circuit-SAT Atom-efficient MWIS Encoding for Rydberg Arrays), a method that provides MWIS encodings of Circuit-SAT instances on the king subgraph geometry of the array. CAMERA represents each logic gate as a compact weighted gadget and assembles the gadgets with a placement and routing compiler inspired by very large scale integration (VLSI) design. On random multi-gate benchmarks, the direct encoding route lowers the atom cost relative to the CNF route by an average factor of $22.4 \pm 1.8$. To demonstrate that the encoding extends from individual weighted gadgets to multi-gate arithmetic blocks, we compile a full adder and a multiplier, verifying each against its complete truth table by exact classical ground state calculations. We further showcase solving a representative Circuit-SAT instance end-to-end, from gate level compilation through a closed-system tensor-network simulation of a hardware-compatible annealing protocol on the encoded 30-atom instance to readout of a satisfying assignment. These results establish a complete encoding and simulation workflow as a proof of principle, and a concrete route toward solving a broader family of combinatorial problems on Rydberg atom arrays.

</details>


### [136] [Geometry versus excitation sector in the decoherence of asymmetric $N$-qubit $W$ states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12965)
*Sougata Bhattacharyya,Sovik Roy,Fatih Ozaydin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究了网络几何和激发态对非对称多部分W态中成对纠缠衰减的独立控制，发现纠缠鲁棒性受网络结构、激发态和噪声对称性的共同影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了分离网络几何与激发态对纠缠衰减的影响，提出可解析处理的N量子比特非对称Lohmayer几何及其互补激发态模型。

Method: 引入N量子比特广义非对称Lohmayer几何模型，区分顶点-基（VB）和基-基（BB）成对类别，推导典型单边噪声下的闭式纠缠动力学。

Result: VB对的纠缠在相同激发态下与对称参考具有相同的噪声依赖性；BB对则表现出同态结构性脆弱，在去极化和幅值阻尼噪声下更易发生纠缠猝死。

Conclusion: 网络几何、激发态和噪声对称性是决定非对称量子网络中成对纠缠鲁棒性的三个独立因素。

Abstract: We investigate how network geometry and excitation sector separately control pairwise entanglement decay in asymmetric multipartite $W$ states. To disentangle these effects, we introduce an analytically tractable $N$-qubit generalization of the asymmetric Lohmayer geometry and its complementary-excitation partner, yielding inequivalent vertex-base (VB) and base-base (BB) pair classes that can be compared directly with symmetric $W$-state references. We derive closed-form concurrence dynamics under representative one-sided noise models and find that, within either excitation sector, the VB concurrence has exactly the same noise dependence as the corresponding symmetric reference, preserving a noise-independent proportional advantage wherever both remain entangled. The amplitude-damping reordering previously identified for the three-qubit Lohmayer state is therefore a cross-sector effect rather than an intrinsic fragility of the VB geometry. In contrast, the BB pair exhibits a genuine same-sector structural fragility, with lower entanglement-sudden-death thresholds than the VB pair under depolarizing noise and, in the $(N-1)$-excitation sector, under amplitude damping. The results establish network geometry, excitation sector, and noise symmetry as distinct ingredients governing pairwise entanglement robustness in asymmetric quantum networks.

</details>


### [137] [Always-on, highly efficient microwave photon detector based on a superconducting artificial molecule](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12972)
*Vyom Kulkarni,Mohammed Ali Aamir,Simon Sundelin,Simone Gasparinetti*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于超导人工分子的连续工作微波单光子探测器，通过亮态捕获光子并转移到长寿命暗态实现高效检测，克服了效率与占空比之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微波单光子探测在量子技术中至关重要，但现有探测器受限于效率、暗计数和连续工作的矛盾，难以同时优化性能指标。

Method: 利用超导人工分子的亮态和暗态，通过驱动-耗散过程将入射光子从亮态转移到暗态，并通过持续监测暗态中的量子跃迁来实现光子探测。

Result: 实现了0.73的循环检测效率和0.47的连续检测效率，瞬时带宽为5 MHz，时间分辨率为1 μs，死区时间为15 μs。

Conclusion: 该方法突破了传统探测器在效率与占空比间的权衡，为量子传感、量子热力学和基础物理提供了可行的连续微波光子探测方案。

Abstract: Efficient detection of single microwave photons is a key capability for emerging quantum technologies. Yet, it remains far less developed than its optical domain counterpart. Realizing detectors that simultaneously achieve high efficiency, low dark counts, and continuous operation has proved challenging. Existing detectors operate cyclically, forcing a trade-off between efficiency and duty cycle. Here, we demonstrate a continuously operated microwave single-photon detector based on a superconducting artificial molecule. In our scheme, an incoming photon is captured by a bright state of the molecule and then transferred to a long-lived dark state via a driven-dissipative process. Photon ``clicks'' are revealed as quantum jumps in the continuously monitored dark state. We observe a cyclic detection efficiency of $0.73$, and a continuous detection efficiency of $0.47$ over a $5\,\mathrm{MHz}$ instantaneous bandwidth, with a $1\,μ\mathrm{s}$ temporal resolution and a $15\,μ\mathrm{s}$ dead time. By overcoming the trade-off between efficiency and duty cycle, this approach establishes continuous microwave photon detection for quantum sensing, quantum thermodynamics, and fundamental physics.

</details>


### [138] [Completeness for flow-preserving rewrite rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13035)
*Miriam Backens,Simon Perdrix*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文扩展了保持流性质的ZX演算重写规则集，并证明了该规则集对于具有适当形式的ZX图之间的所有流保持转换是完备的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了实现对量子计算的完全图形化推理，需要完整的图形重写规则集；特别是保持流性质的ZX-演算重写规则在测量基量子计算中具有重要意义。

Method: 通过引入新的规则和扩展已有规则，构建一个增强的流保持重写规则集，并利用显式保持流的方式进行电路提取等价证明。

Result: 提出了新的流保持重写规则并扩展了现有规则，证明了所得到的规则集对于具备适当形式的ZX图间的流保持变换是完备的。

Conclusion: 扩展后的流保持ZX-演算重写规则集是完备的，可用于确定性实现测量基量子计算中的图形化推理。

Abstract: Complete sets of graphical rewrite rules enable fully graphical reasoning about quantum computations and have been an area of active research for more than a decade. Many recent applications of the ZX-calculus have made use of the close correspondence between ZX-diagrams and computations in the one-way model of measurement-based quantum computation. In this model, various kinds of flow properties ensure deterministic implementability; for ZX-diagrams, these same properties allow efficient translation into quantum circuits (a problem that is known to be #P-hard in general). Therefore, flow-preserving ZX-calculus rewrite rules are of strong interest.
  Here, we extend the set of flow-preserving rules appearing in the literature with a few new rules and extensions of existing rules. We then show that the resulting rule set is complete for all flow-preserving translations between ZX-diagrams of appropriate form. The proof employs a manifestly flow-preserving equivalent of circuit extraction, where a diagram with gflow is transformed, using only flow-preserving rewrite rules, into a diagram with causal flow.

</details>


### [139] [Time evolution of nonlinear dynamics on a quantum processor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13041)
*José Diogo da Costa Jesus,Abhishek Setty,Tommaso Calarco,Dieter Jaksch,Francisco Cárdenas López,Felix Motzoi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次在量子处理器上实验实现了非线性流体动力学的时间演化，采用变分量子算法直接编码非线性动力学，避免了传统线性化方法的开销，并通过零噪声外推技术克服硬件噪声，成功重构了多时间步的演化过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展量子计算至非线性偏微分方程模拟面临根本挑战，因传统量子模拟基于线性酉演化，难以处理非线性和非厄米系统。本文旨在突破这一限制，实现对非线性流体方程（如Burgers方程）的量子模拟。

Method: 采用混合变分量子框架，将粘性和无粘Burgers方程的演化编码到参数化量子电路中，通过量子-经典优化迭代重构时变场；引入无电路折叠开销的零噪声外推方法，以应对硬件噪声和有限相干时间。

Result: 成功演示了雷诺数达10²量级的对流主导动力学，在含大量纠缠门的深层电路中准确执行时间演化，克服了设备噪声影响，实现了多个时间步的精确重构。

Conclusion: 这是目前已知首个在量子处理器上实现非线性时间传播的实验，突破了传统量子模拟局限于线性系统的限制，为量子计算应用于非线性连续介质动力学开辟了新路径。

Abstract: From fluid flow and transport to collective dynamics, numerical simulation of nonlinear partial differential equations underpins modern scientific computing. Extending this capability to quantum computers remains a longstanding challenge because nonlinear and non-Hermitian evolution is fundamentally incompatible with conventional Hamiltonian-based quantum simulation. Here we experimentally realize the time evolution of nonlinear fluid dynamics on a quantum processor using a hybrid variational framework for the viscous and inviscid Burgers equations. Our approach directly encodes the nonlinear dynamics into a variational optimization procedure, avoiding the enlarged linear embeddings and truncation overhead associated with Carleman linearization-based quantum algorithms. We further demonstrate convection-dominated dynamics corresponding to Reynolds numbers of order $10^2$. We encode the governing evolution into parametrized quantum circuits and iteratively reconstruct the time-dependent field through quantum-classical optimization. By introducing a zero-noise extrapolation method without additional circuit-folding overhead, we accurately execute deep error-circuits with entangling-gate counts beyond those typical of Hadamard test circuits. We accurately reconstruct the time evolution across multiple timesteps despite hardware noise and finite device coherence. Our results constitute, to our knowledge, the first experimental realization of nonlinear time propagation on a quantum processor, extending quantum simulation beyond predominantly linear settings and establishing a route toward quantum computation for nonlinear continuum dynamics.

</details>


### [140] [Quantifying nonclassicality in qubit systems via positive operator-valued measures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13088)
*Abdul Sattar Khan,Mehdi Abdi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于序列测量中Kolmogorov一致性条件违背的量子非经典性操作度量，通过POVM的非锐度参数量化非经典性，并揭示了非投影测量在对角初态下也能展现非经典性，最大值为1/4，连接了Kolmogorov一致性、Leggett-Garg不等式与POVM，具有实验可实现性和量子技术认证应用潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作通过NCGD映射或测量扰动下的量子关联刻画经典性，但缺乏直接可操作的非经典性度量；本文旨在建立一个基于测量过程本身统计一致性的、可实验检测的非经典性量化工具。

Method: 基于序列测量中的Kolmogorov一致性条件构建非经典性判据，引入POVM的非锐度参数az进行量化分析，推导不同初态（含相干态）下的判据表达式，并分析其在投影测量与非投影测量下的行为差异。

Result: 对于对角初态和投影测量，该判据恒为零；使用非投影POVM时可探测到非经典性；判据最大值为1/4，出现在无偏POVM、最大去相位和等初态布居条件下；建立了Kolmogorov一致性、Leggett-Garg不等式与POVM之间的联系。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个可操作且实验可行的非经典性度量方法，表明非投影测量在揭示量子系统非经典行为中的关键作用，为量子技术的认证提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: We introduce an operational measure of nonclassicality for qubit systems based on the violation of Kolmogorov consistency conditions in sequential measurements. In contrast to previous work by Milz et al.~\cite{Milz-2020}, who characterized classicality via NCGD maps, and Sakuldee et al.~\cite{Sakuldee-2022a, Sakuldee-2022b}, who studied quantum correlations under measurement disturbance, our witness explicitly quantifies nonclassicality in terms of the POVM unsharpness parameter $a_z$. For projective measurements on an initially diagonal state, the witness vanishes identically, showing that such measurements cannot reveal nonclassicality. However, by generalizing to positive operator-valued measures (POVMs), we find that non-projective measurements can reveal nonclassicality even for diagonal initial states. We derive explicit expressions for the witness for general initial states, including coherences, and show that its maximum value is $1/4$, which is a new result achieved for unbiased POVMs, maximal dephasing, and equal initial populations. Our results connect Kolmogorov consistency, Leggett-Garg inequalities, and POVMs, providing an experimentally accessible tool for detecting nonclassicality in qubit systems with potential applications in quantum technology certification.

</details>


### [141] [Robust controlled-Z gate for Rydberg atoms based on level-crossing-free echoing rapid adiabatic passage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13090)
*Yichi Zhang,Zhenqi Bai,Xu Zhao,Hongyan Fan,Ximo Wang,Tiecheng Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于无能级交叉回声快速绝热布居转移的里德堡原子受控-Z门方案，具有高保真度和抗噪声能力，适用于大规模中性原子阵列中的并行门操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了实现高保真度、鲁棒性强且适用于大规模中性原子量子计算的两比特和三比特量子逻辑门，避免传统方法中的能级交叉和动力学相位积累问题。

Method: 设计了反对称拉比频率脉冲和对称失谐脉冲，通过时间反演对称的双脉冲序列实现无能级交叉的演化，并自然消除动力学相位；使用林德布拉德主方程考虑耗散效应。

Result: 数值模拟显示两比特CZ门保真度达0.9999，在±2%拉比频率波动和±1%失谐偏移下仍保持在0.999以上；三比特CCZ门保真度为0.999，在单参数波动不超过±3%时保真度始终高于0.997。

Conclusion: 该方案无需激光相位跳变或快速开关操作，零面积脉冲结构抑制一阶强度噪声，对称双脉冲序列避免空间分辨激光切换，适合大规模中性原子阵列的并行量子门实现。

Abstract: We propose a controlled-Z gate scheme for Rydberg atoms based on level-crossing-free echoing rapid adiabatic population transfer. We design antisymmetric Rabi frequency pulses and symmetric detuning pulses, enabling the system to completely avoid level-crossing points throughout the evolution, and the dynamical phase is naturally eliminated by the time-reversal symmetry of the double-pulse sequence. We incorporate dissipative effects through the Lindblad master equation. The numerical simulation yields a two-qubit CZ gate fidelity of 0.9999. When the Rabi-frequency fluctuation is within $\pm 2\%$, and the detuning offset is within $\pm 1\%$, the fidelity can still remain above 0.999. Under the same dissipative model, the three-qubit CCZ gate achieves a fidelity of 0.999. When a single-parameter fluctuation does not exceed $\pm 3\%$, the fidelity is always higher than 0.997. Our scheme requires no laser phase jumps or fast switching operations. The zero-area pulse structure suppresses first-order intensity noise, and the symmetric double-pulse sequence avoids spatially resolved laser switching, making it suitable for parallel gate operations in large-scale neutral-atom arrays.

</details>


### [142] [Time-resolved correlation engineering in DLCZ Raman photon sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13091)
*Jiun-Shiuan Shiu,Chang-Wei Lin,Chi-Ming Yang,Ite A. Yu,Yong-Fan Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种包含传播效应和开放系统动力学的量子理论，用于描述DLCZ型光子源中的时间相关性和关联特性，并通过实验验证了其关键预测，实现了对反斯托克斯波包的时间调控与关联工程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缺乏一个统一的、能够连接控制参数与实际传播和噪声条件下关联特性的理论模型来描述DLCZ协议中的光子源行为。

Method: 结合海森堡-朗之万动力学与麦克斯韦-薛定谔传播方程，构建了一个包含写入引起的人口重分布和传播效应的开放系统量子理论，并在实验上验证其预测。

Result: 理论成功预测了时间依赖的斯托克斯光产生、自旋波演化、可检索的反斯托克斯波包以及时间分辨的交叉关联；实验确认了在不同写入脉冲条件下关联的增强行为及可控的时间门控与切片操作。

Conclusion: 该理论为内存兼容的DLCZ光子源提供了定量的关联工程框架，支持经典控制下的时间波包调控，推动了记忆辅助量子网络中光源的设计与优化。

Abstract: Memory-assisted quantum networks require photon sources with controllable temporal and correlation properties. The Duan-Lukin-Cirac-Zoller (DLCZ) protocol provides a platform based on spontaneous Raman scattering in atomic ensembles, but a unified predictive theory connecting control parameters to correlations under realistic propagation and noise conditions remains lacking. Here we present a propagation-inclusive open-system quantum theory that retains write-induced population redistribution while combining Heisenberg-Langevin dynamics with Maxwell-Schrödinger propagation. We experimentally validate its key predictions. The theory predicts time-dependent Stokes generation, spin-wave evolution, retrieved anti-Stokes wavepackets, and time-resolved cross-correlations. Experiments confirm robust correlations under retrieval tuning and enhanced correlations for shorter write pulses, consistent with the different scaling of correlated coincidences and accidental backgrounds with the mean spin-wave excitation number. Classically controlled retrieval enables temporal gating and slicing of the anti-Stokes wavepacket, establishing a quantitative framework for correlation engineering in memory-compatible DLCZ photon sources.

</details>


### [143] [Formal Verification of Quantum Ancilla Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13099)
*Jiqi Li,Jingyi Mei,Wang Fang,Ji Guan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种端到端的量子电路辅助比特安全性验证与修复框架，通过两步约简策略高效检测并修复干净和脏辅助比特的安全性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确保辅助比特（ancilla qubit）的安全性是量子编译中的关键正确性要求，尤其是对于携带未知初态的脏辅助比特，其形式化验证因状态空间随比特数指数增长而极具挑战。

Method: 提出一种两步约简策略：首先将m量子比特脏辅助寄存器的安全性验证分解为2m个独立的干净辅助比特安全性检查；然后将每个干净辅助比特安全性问题转化为针对Pauli-Z和Pauli-X算子的代数交换性检查，并设计基于局部单量子比特旋转的轻量级修复机制。

Result: 实验表明该方法可扩展至数千量子比特，能有效发现逻辑错误和相位错误，并通过添加局部旋转门修复多种本地辅助比特故障，同时保持电路功能不变。

Conclusion: 该框架为清洁和脏辅助比特提供了高效、可并行的验证与修复方案，显著提升了大规模量子电路中辅助比特管理的可靠性与实用性。

Abstract: Ensuring ancilla safety is a critical correctness requirement for quantum compilation, since ancilla qubits are routinely introduced to implement complex operations with fewer gates and reduced depth. However, formally verifying this property is computationally hard due to state-space explosion in the number of qubits, particularly for dirty ancillae, which carry unknown initial states and must be restored after use. We propose an end-to-end verification-and-repair framework that rigorously addresses both clean and dirty ancilla safety. Our core contribution is a two-step reduction strategy: we first prove that verifying an $m$-qubit dirty ancilla register decomposes into $2m$ independent clean ancilla safety checks; subsequently, we reduce each clean ancilla safety instance to an algebraic commutativity check against Pauli-$Z$ and Pauli-$X$ operators. This approach yields an efficient and naturally parallel verifier and enables actionable diagnosis by classifying violations into logic errors and phase errors. Leveraging this diagnosis, we further design lightweight repair routines that append local single-qubit rotations to eliminate a broad class of local ancilla faults. We implement the full pipeline in a prototype tool using a dual-backend architecture combining decision diagrams and weighted model counting, and validate it on diverse circuits ranging from arithmetic benchmarks to Grover's algorithm. Our experiments demonstrate scalability to thousands of qubits and show that the proposed repairs effectively improve ancilla safety while preserving circuit functionality.

</details>


### [144] [Controlling quantum transport by measurement-rate modulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13109)
*Jesús Casado-Pascual,Luis Octavio Castaños-Cervantes*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 通过测量速率的时间调制，研究了一个可精确求解的连续监测量子粒子模型，揭示了周期性和随机切换下的长时电流行为，并发现了测量诱导的芝诺与反芝诺输运机制之间的 crossover。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索通过调节测量速率来控制开放量子系统动力学的机制。

Method: 基于一个在切换晶格上的连续监测量子粒子的可精确求解模型，推导出周期性和随机切换下长时电流的解析表达式。

Result: 发现周期性和随机切换具有共同的慢切换极限，并识别出由测量诱导的芝诺与反芝诺输运机制之间的 crossover，该机制可调控电流方向。

Conclusion: 测量速率的时间调制是一种有效调控开放量子系统中输运行为的强大手段。

Abstract: Temporal modulation of the measurement rate provides a powerful mechanism for controlling open-system dynamics through measurement backaction. We demonstrate this mechanism in a minimal exactly solvable model of a continuously monitored quantum particle on a switching lattice. We derive exact analytical expressions for the long-time current under both periodic and stochastic switching, revealing a common slow-switching limit and identifying a measurement-induced crossover between Zeno- and anti-Zeno-dominated transport regimes that controls the direction of the current.

</details>


### [145] [Classical Simulation and Design Frontiers for IBM's Doped Clifford Sampling Experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13110)
*Hidetaka Manabe,Hanfeng Gu,Feng Pan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种确定性的时域边界张量网络收缩方法，用于经典模拟IBM的70量子比特掺杂Clifford随机电路采样实验，通过高效计算精确振幅，验证了实验结果的保真度，并展示了该方法在诊断实验输出和设计未来实验中的实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了验证IBM掺杂Clifford电路实验结果的准确性，并提供一种高效的经典模拟方法来评估量子优势声明。

Method: 设计了一种针对一维砖砌结构开放边界的张量网络收缩算法，利用算符施密特秩为2的纠缠门特性，实现低收缩宽度的精确振幅计算，并采用分布式GPU加速计算。

Result: 成功在37.3分钟内完成2051个振幅批次的计算，最大中间张量为2^35个复数元素（256 GiB），分布在32个节点共256块H100 GPU上，得到log-XEB估计值为0.35034，与IBM的保真度下限一致。

Conclusion: 所提方法能有效模拟大规模掺杂Clifford电路，为实验验证提供了实用工具，并可用于未来量子采样实验的设计与评估。

Abstract: We classically simulate the IBM doped Clifford random circuit sampling experiment, comprising $70$ qubits, $70$ entangling layers, and $468$ inserted $T$ gates. A deterministic temporal-boundary tensor network contraction approach is specifically designed to tackle such open-boundary one-dimensional brickwork circuits with operator-Schmidt-rank-$2$ entangling gates. For an $n$-qubit circuit of depth $d$, the resulting unsliced path evaluates an exact amplitude with contraction width $\lceil d/2\rceil$; Ratcatcher calculations certify that no smaller width is possible for the tested instances. Because one-qubit gates are absorbed without changing the network topology, the width and dense scheduled contraction cost are independent of their values and of the number and placement of $T$ gates. For the IBM instance, its largest intermediate tensor contains $2^{35}$ complex64 entries (256 times smaller than IBM's estimation), corresponding to a tensor payload of $256$ GiB, and is distributed across eight GPUs within a node. Using 32 nodes, with eight NVIDIA H100 GPUs per node, we completed all 2051 amplitude batches corresponding to IBM's published output bitstrings in 37.3 minutes. The resulting probabilities yield a log-XEB estimate of $0.35034$ with a 95\% interval of $[0.29763,0.40305]$. Under the Porter--Thomas and scrambled-noise assumptions, this is numerically compatible with IBM's fidelity lower bound; separately, fidelity-weighted resource accounting projects a 583-contraction workload with a 10.6-minute makespan on the same 32 nodes. More broadly, the approach provides a practical diagnostic for experimental outputs and a quantitative tool for designing future doped Clifford sampling experiments.

</details>


### [146] [Controlled dynamics of a multi-component discrete-time quantum walker](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13161)
*Vikash Mittal,Tomasz Sowiński*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究了一维格点上三组分量子粒子的离散时间量子行走，利用Gell-Mann矩阵生成的参数化旋转作为硬币算符，系统探索了不同参数下位置空间概率分布和输运行为，揭示了从快速扩散到强各向异性及部分局域化的多种动力学 regime。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索多组分量子行走中内部组分间耦合对动力学行为的影响，寻求对量子扩散与局域化行为的调控途径。

Method: 采用由Gell-Mann矩阵生成的参数化硬币算符，对三组分离散时间量子行走进行数值模拟，系统扫描参数空间，分析位置空间概率分布及均值与方差的比值。

Result: 发现通过调节硬币参数可实现多种输运行为，包括近对称扩散、强各向异性传播以及部分局域化现象。

Conclusion: 该框架为设计和调控多组分量子行走中的定向扩散与捕获行为提供了新方法。

Abstract: We investigate a discrete-time quantum walk of a three-component quantum particle on a one-dimensional lattice. As coin operators, we employ parameterized rotations generated by the Gell-Mann matrices, which enable systematic tuning of the couplings between the internal components. We analyze how these couplings influence and control the dynamics by systematically exploring the position-space probability distribution across a broad region of the parameter space. To quantify the impact of different inter-component couplings, we further examine the ratio of the mean position to the variance in each half of the lattice. Our results indicates that the system supports a rich variety of transport regimes, ranging from nearly symmetric, rapidly spreading walks to strongly anisotropic dynamics with partial localization. This framework thus provides a new avenue for engineering targeted spreading and trapping behavior in multicomponent discrete-time quantum walks.

</details>


### [147] [Witnessing the architecture of quantum circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13169)
*Raphaël Mothe,Otfried Gühne*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于认证量子电路架构与目标幺正变换不兼容的通用框架，通过半定规划构造量子电路架构见证，并利用稳定子形式简化克利福德幺正门的情况为线性规划，从而提供不可实现性的严格证明及资源下界估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现有量子电路综合与编译方法缺乏对目标幺正变换无法在给定架构中实现的严格认证问题，提供非构造性的资源需求下界判断工具。

Method: 将见证构造表述为半定规划问题，通过最大化目标幺正变换的Choi态与测试电路Choi态之间的保真度来构建见证；对于克利福德幺正门，利用稳定子形式将其转化为线性规划以提高效率。

Result: 实现了对最多约七个两量子比特门规模电路的有效数值认证，获得了某些任意规模架构族的解析见证，能够给出门数量或电路深度等资源的下界，并可用于实验上验证实际设备超越特定架构能力。

Conclusion: 该框架为量子电路架构提供了实用且定量的不兼容性认证工具，兼具理论分析与实验基准测试价值，尤其在克利福德电路中可通过线性规划高效实现。

Abstract: Determining whether a target unitary can be implemented within a prescribed quantum circuit architecture is a fundamental problem in quantum information, with direct implications for optimisation and compilation of quantum circuits, and hardware-efficient quantum computation. While existing synthesis and compilation methods are primarily constructive, they generally do not provide rigorous certificates that a unitary cannot be realised using given implementation resources. Here we introduce a general framework to define quantum circuit architecture witnesses, which certify the incompatibility of a unitary transformation with a specified quantum circuit architecture. We formulate the witness construction as a semidefinite program by maximising the fidelity between the Choi state of the target unitary and those of tested circuits. The resulting witnesses provide practical and quantitative certificates of incompatibility, implying lower bounds on implementation resources such as the gate count or circuit depth, and can also be used experimentally to benchmark quantum devices by certifying that an implemented unitary channel goes beyond the capabilities of a given circuit architecture. For Clifford unitaries, we exploit the stabiliser formalism to reduce the construction to linear programming, enabling both more efficient numerical certification for circuits containing on the order of seven two-qubit gates, and analytical witnesses for some families of architectures made of an arbitrary number of gates.

</details>


### [148] [Entanglement distribution and quantum storage of more than 8000 modes over a metropolitan network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13177)
*Angelo Gelmini Rodriguez,Louis Nicolas,Théo Sanchez Mejia,Pavel Sekatski,Nicolas Brunner,Towsif Taher,Rob Thew,Philippe Goldner,Mikael Afzelius*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 实现了电信波段光子与物质之间的纠缠，并利用多模量子存储器在光纤网络中分发纠缠，展示了量子中继节点的实用化潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了实现基于光纤的量子中继器中的高效通信，需要生成远程纠缠并提高通信速率，而复用技术结合多模量子存储器是关键。

Method: 使用掺杂$^{171}$Yb$^{3+}$:Y$_2$SiO$_5$的多模量子存储器，结合带宽匹配的纠缠光子对源，通过施密特分解量化有效时间模式容量，在实验室和城市光纤网络中演示纠缠分发。

Result: 实现了25.3 km光纤中 telecom 光子与979 nm 存储光子的纠缠，存储时间达125 μs，覆盖16340个时间模式；并在实地部署中通过5.66 km 城市光纤网络分发纠缠，存储8235个模式达63 μs。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了一个具备高时间多模能力的量子中继节点，为实际应用中的长距离量子通信提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Entanglement generation between telecommunication photons and matter is central to fibre-based quantum repeaters. Achieving practical communication rates requires multiplexing, which multimode quantum memories can provide. Rare-earth-ion ensembles offer large temporal multimode storage by exploiting the numerous spectral channels within their absorption spectrum. Here, we report on a quantum repeater node comprised of a $^{171}$Yb$^{3+}$:Y$_2$SiO$_5$ multimode quantum memory, featuring a 250 MHz bandwidth and a $76.6~μ\mathrm{s}$ lifetime, and a bandwidth-matched entangled photon-pair source. We introduce and validate a quantitative measure of the effective temporal mode capacity using a Schmidt decomposition. With this platform, we demonstrate entanglement between a telecom photon propagating through a 25.3 km fiber spool and a 979 nm photon stored for $125~μ\mathrm{s}$ across 16340 temporal modes. Finally, we report a field deployment distributing entanglement over 5.66 km through the Geneva metropolitan fibre network while storing 8235 modes for $63~μ\mathrm{s}$.

</details>


### [149] [Mid-circuit ground-state cooling and ancilla readout in the $\textit{omg}$ architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13181)
*Sean Brudney,Connor Burns,Gabriel J. Gregory,Evan Ritchie,David J. Wineland,David T. C. Allcock,Jameson O'Reilly*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于囚禁离子的光学亚稳态-基态（omg）架构，能够在无需额外硬件开销的情况下实现双物种实验的全部功能，如共轭冷却和非破坏性辅助读出。实验验证了可通过操控基态（g）量子比特将混合态库仑晶体的全局运动模式冷却至基态，同时保持亚稳态（m）量子比特的相干性，从而实现利用g比特荧光检测对m比特状态的非破坏性读取。该技术有望扩展应用于抑制离子输运后的运动加热以及量子纠错中的综合征提取，是迈向容错量子计算的重要步骤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在不增加硬件复杂度的前提下实现类似双物种离子系统的功能，如高效冷却和非破坏性测量，推动 trapped-ion 量子计算机的发展。

Method: 利用 dissipative 操作对混合亚稳态-基态库仑晶体的全局运动模式进行冷却，并通过量子逻辑光谱学实现对亚稳态量子比特的非破坏性读出。

Result: 成功将混合态离子晶体的运动模式冷却至基态，同时保持了亚稳态量子比特的量子相干性，并实现了基于荧光检测的非破坏性读出。

Conclusion: omg 架构为未来大规模、容错的 trapped-ion 量子计算机提供了可行的技术路径，尤其在离子输运和量子纠错方面具有重要应用潜力。

Abstract: The trapped-ion optical-metastable-ground ($\textit{omg}$) architecture for quantum processors promises the full functionality of two-species experiments, including sympathetic cooling and non-destructive ancilla readout, without the corresponding hardware overhead. We confirm that we can cool a global motional mode of a mixed metastable-ground state Coulomb crystal to the motional ground state via dissipative operations on the ground ($\textit{g}$) qubit without disturbing coherence of the metastable ($\textit{m}$) qubit. This enables quantum logic spectroscopy to non-destructively readout the state of the $\textit{m}$ qubit using fluorescence detection of the $\textit{g}$ qubit. Extensions of these demonstrations to larger system sizes should enable the mitigation of motional heating after ion shuttling and syndrome extraction for quantum error correction, both crucial primitives for future fault-tolerant quantum computers based on trapped ions.

</details>


### [150] [Counter-examples for Tensorization Property of Strong Data Processing Inequality for Quantum Divergences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13204)
*Yu Cao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子强数据处理不等式（SDPI）的张量化性质，发现其在某些量子chi-square散度和量子相对熵情况下不成立，给出了两个否定结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管经典信息论中强数据处理不等式的张量化对一大类f-散度成立，但其量子对应情形尚不清楚，本文旨在探究量子散度中SDPI张量化的普遍性质。

Method: 通过分析特定类型的量子chi-square散度和量子相对熵，检验其是否满足张量化性质，并给出反例证明其不成立。

Result: 发现SDPI的张量化性质在某些量子chi-square散度中失效，且同样不适用于量子相对熵。

Conclusion: 量子散度中的SDPI张量化性质并不普遍成立，这与经典情形形成对比，揭示了量子信息处理中新的局限性。

Abstract: The data processing inequality is a fundamental property that describes the loss of information through noisy channels. A more refined description is characterized by the strong data processing inequality (SDPI). In classical information theory, the tensorization of strong data processing inequality holds for a whole family of $f$-divergences. However, its quantum counterpart is less known. The tensorization of SDPI was shown only for some special cases previously, and the general understanding about the tensorization property of SDPI for quantum divergences remains open. In this work, we report two negative results: the tensorization property fails for certain quantum chi-square divergences, and it also does not hold for the quantum relative entropy.

</details>


### [151] [Ion trap on borosilicate substrate with integrated femtosecond-laser-written waveguide](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13207)
*Jakob Wahl,Alexander Zesar,Philipp Hurdax,Marco Schmauser,Victoria Schwab,Michael Pasquini,Marco Valentini,Clemens Rössler,Thomas Monz,Bernhard Lamprecht,Klemens Schüppert,Philipp Schindler*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于硼硅酸盐玻璃的离子阱平台，集成了飞秒激光直写波导用于片上光传输，实现了低损耗单模导光和弯曲波导，并在低温捕获离子系统中验证了其功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为实现可扩展的离子阱量子设备中的集成光传输，需要一种兼容工业制造、具有低损耗且可扩展的片上光路方案。

Method: 在硼硅酸盐玻璃上构建离子阱，并通过飞秒激光直写技术在独立光学层中制造单模低损耗波导，该层与电极基底分离并键合于陷阱之上；同时优化波导设计以支持729 nm和405 nm波长的单模传输，并结合活性光纤对准与键合工艺。

Result: 实现了729 nm和405 nm波长下的单模低损耗传输，最小曲率半径达6 mm；成功演示了Ca+离子的捕获、输运及通过集成波导进行的相干操作；测量到介电材料暴露引起的缓慢漂移的杂散电场。

Conclusion: 该平台兼容硅基集成和工业制造流程，提供了一条稳定且可扩展的集成光传输路径，适用于未来大规模离子阱量子器件，并可结合混合微光学元件实现单离子寻址。

Abstract: We present an ion-trap platform on borosilicate glass with an integrated femtosecond-laser-written waveguide for on-chip light delivery. The optical layer is physically separated from the electrode substrate and bonded atop the trap, remaining compatible with silicon-based integration. We engineer single-mode low-loss guidance at 729 nm with tunable mode-field diameter and achieve low-loss curved waveguides down to a radius of curvature of 6 mm. We also extend single-mode operation to a wavelength of 405 nm. The fabrication process is compatible with the industrial fabrication of a single-metal-layer surface-electrode trap, including active fiber alignment and bonding. We validate the platform in a cryogenic trapped-ion system with $^{40}$Ca$^+$, demonstrating trapping, shuttling the ion to a zone in front of the waveguide, and coherent operations driven by 729 nm light delivered through the integrated waveguide. We characterize the effect of the exposed dielectric on the ion and measure stray electric fields that show slow drift at a timescale of hours. The architecture is compatible with hybrid micro-optics (e.g. pick-and-place lenses) to realize single ion addressing and provides a robust, scalable route to integrated light delivery for trapped-ion devices.

</details>


### [152] [Heterogeneously Integrated Squeezed-Light Generation and Detection on a Single Photonic Chip](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13218)
*Haoran Chen,Benjamin Westcott,Fatemehsadat Tabatabaei,Xiangwen Guo,Shuman Sun,Zijiao Yang,Gedalia Y. Koehler,Beichen Wang,Shadrach Sarpong,Steven Bowers,Olivier Pfister,Andreas Beling,Xu Yi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 实现了在同一光子芯片上集成压缩光生成、路由和平衡零差检测，通过异质集成技术展示了一个包含34个量子模式的双模压缩量子微梳，压缩度约为3 dB，为全集成压缩光量子光子系统提供了可扩展架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决压缩光生成与光电探测在材料需求上的根本冲突，实现低光学损耗以保持量子关联，同时高效吸收光子进行探测。

Method: 采用异质集成技术，在同一光子芯片上集成压缩光生成和平衡零差检测，利用非线性波导产生量子微梳，并通过紧凑的光电探测器实现片上测量。

Result: 成功实现了约3 dB的压缩度，展示了包含34个量子模式的双模压缩量子微梳的片上生成与检测。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一种可扩展的全集成压缩光量子光子系统架构，统一了量子态的生成、处理与检测，推动了量子传感与连续变量量子信息处理的发展。

Abstract: Squeezed light underpins quantum-enhanced sensing and continuous-variable quantum information processing, and integrated photonics offers a route to producing it at scale. Universal to these applications are squeezed-light generation and measurement. Importantly, quantum measurements serve not only as readout but also as active operations in quantum-state evolution. However, integrating squeezed-light generation and photodetection on the same photonic chip has remained challenging because they impose fundamentally conflicting material requirements: low optical loss to preserve quantum correlations, but efficient photon absorption for photodetection. Here, we demonstrate squeezed-light generation, routing, and balanced homodyne detection integrated on a single photonic chip through heterogeneous integration. A two-mode squeezed quantum microcomb comprising 34 quantum modes is measured with approximately 3 dB squeezing. Our work establishes a scalable architecture for fully integrated squeezed-light quantum photonic systems, unifying quantum-state generation, processing, and detection on a single chip.

</details>
